`src/app/session.rs`:

```rs
//! Attached game, its compatibility guard and address resolution.

use std::collections::HashMap;
use std::path::Path;
use std::sync::Arc;
use std::time::{Duration, Instant};

use crate::aob;
use crate::inject;
use crate::mem::{Module, Process};
use crate::pointer::{self, Chain, Parsed};
use crate::profile::{self, Calibration, CheatDef, GameProfile, Locator};
use crate::scan::VType;

/// Modules of anti-cheat systems. FahrenHack refuses to touch such games:
/// it is for single-player games only and never tries to get around protection.
const ANTI_CHEAT: &[(&str, &str)] = &[
    ("easyanticheat", "Easy Anti-Cheat"),
    ("beclient", "BattlEye"),
    ("battleye", "BattlEye"),
    ("eaanticheat", "EA AntiCheat"),
    ("gameguard", "nProtect GameGuard"),
    ("npggnt", "nProtect GameGuard"),
    ("xigncode", "XIGNCODE3"),
    ("x3.xem", "XIGNCODE3"),
    ("faceit", "FACEIT"),
    ("acesdk", "ACE"),
    ("anticheatexpert", "ACE"),
];

fn anti_cheat(modules: &[Module]) -> Option<&'static str> {
    modules.iter().find_map(|m| {
        let n = m.name.to_lowercase();
        ANTI_CHEAT
            .iter()
            .find(|(k, _)| n.contains(k))
            .map(|(_, name)| *name)
    })
}

pub(super) enum Active {
    Freeze(Vec<u8>),
    Patch { addr: usize, original: Vec<u8> },
    /// Code injection: the site jumps into `cave`; `original` is restored when switched off.
    Inject { site: usize, original: Vec<u8>, cave: usize },
}

impl Active {
    /// Code range this effect has overwritten, to refuse overlapping patches.
    pub(super) fn code_range(&self) -> Option<(usize, usize)> {
        match self {
            Active::Patch { addr, original } => Some((*addr, addr + original.len())),
            Active::Inject { site, original, .. } => Some((*site, site + original.len())),
            Active::Freeze(_) => None,
        }
    }
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub(super) enum Unresolved {
    NeedsCalibration,
    Waiting,
    Missing,
    Invalid,
}

impl Unresolved {
    pub(super) fn code(self) -> &'static str {
        match self {
            Unresolved::NeedsCalibration => "calibrate",
            Unresolved::Waiting => "waiting",
            Unresolved::Missing => "missing",
            Unresolved::Invalid => "invalid",
        }
    }

    pub(super) fn message(self) -> &'static str {
        match self {
            Unresolved::NeedsCalibration => "Чит ещё не настроен: нажмите «Настроить»",
            Unresolved::Waiting => "Адрес пока недоступен: загрузите сохранение и войдите в игру",
            Unresolved::Missing => "Сигнатура не найдена: профиль не подходит к этой версии игры",
            Unresolved::Invalid => "Ошибка в описании чита",
        }
    }
}

pub(super) struct GameSession {
    pub(super) proc: Arc<Process>,
    pub(super) modules: Vec<Module>,
    pub(super) main: Module,
    pub(super) build: String,
    pub(super) profile: GameProfile,
    /// The game has a ready-made profile.
    pub(super) known: bool,
    pub(super) calib: Calibration,
    /// Addresses found by calibration in this session (used when no pointer chain is known).
    pub(super) session_addresses: HashMap<String, usize>,
    pub(super) active: HashMap<String, Active>,
    /// Code caves allocated in the game for injection cheats (kept for the whole session).
    pub(super) caves: HashMap<String, usize>,
    chains: HashMap<String, Vec<Parsed>>,
    pick: HashMap<String, (usize, Instant)>,
    aob_cache: HashMap<String, Option<usize>>,
}

impl GameSession {
    pub(super) fn open(
        pid: u32,
        exe: &str,
        profiles: &[GameProfile],
        data_dir: &Path,
    ) -> Result<Self, String> {
        let p = Process::open(pid, exe)?;
        let modules = p.modules();
        if let Some(ac) = anti_cheat(&modules) {
            return Err(format!(
                "В игре работает античит ({ac}). FahrenHack подключается только к одиночным играм без античита: иначе можно получить блокировку."
            ));
        }
        let main = modules
            .iter()
            .find(|m| m.name.eq_ignore_ascii_case(exe))
            .or(modules.first())
            .cloned()
            .ok_or("Не удалось прочитать модули процесса: игра ещё загружается или это 32-битная система")?;
        let build = p.build_key(&main);
        let found = profiles
            .iter()
            .find(|profile| {
                profile
                    .processes
                    .iter()
                    .any(|name| name.eq_ignore_ascii_case(exe))
            })
            .cloned();
        let known = found.is_some();
        let mut profile = found.unwrap_or_else(|| GameProfile::generic(exe));
        profile
            .cheats
            .extend(profile::load_custom(data_dir, &profile.id));
        let calib = Calibration::load(data_dir, &profile.id, &build);
        let mut game = Self {
            proc: Arc::new(p),
            modules,
            main,
            build,
            profile,
            known,
            calib,
            session_addresses: HashMap::new(),
            active: HashMap::new(),
            caves: HashMap::new(),
            chains: HashMap::new(),
            pick: HashMap::new(),
            aob_cache: HashMap::new(),
        };
        game.rebuild_chains();
        Ok(game)
    }

    pub(super) fn restore_patches(&mut self) {
        for active in std::mem::take(&mut self.active).into_values() {
            match active {
                Active::Patch { addr, original } => {
                    self.proc.write(addr, &original);
                }
                Active::Inject { site, original, .. } => {
                    self.proc.write(site, &original);
                }
                Active::Freeze(_) => {}
            }
        }
    }

    /// Name of another active cheat that already rewrote code in `[site, site+len)`.
    pub(super) fn code_conflict(&self, site: usize, len: usize, except: &str) -> Option<String> {
        self.active
            .iter()
            .filter(|(id, _)| id.as_str() != except)
            .find(|(_, a)| a.code_range().is_some_and(|(s, e)| site < e && site + len > s))
            .map(|(id, _)| self.def(id).map_or_else(|| id.clone(), |d| d.name.clone()))
    }

    /// Switches a code injection on. `param` is written to the cave's data slot first,
    /// so the injected code never runs with a stale parameter.
    pub(super) fn inject_on(&mut self, def: &CheatDef, param: Option<&[u8]>) -> Result<(), String> {
        let Locator::Inject { size, code, .. } = &def.locator else {
            return Err("Этот чит не использует инъекцию кода".into());
        };
        if let Some(Active::Inject { cave, .. }) = self.active.get(&def.id) {
            if let Some(p) = param {
                self.proc.write(cave + inject::PARAM_OFFSET, p);
            }
            return Ok(());
        }
        let site = self.resolve(&def.id).map_err(|e| e.message().to_string())?;
        if let Some(other) = self.code_conflict(site, *size, &def.id) {
            return Err(format!("Этот чит меняет тот же код, что и «{other}»: сначала выключите его"));
        }
        let parts = inject::parse(code).ok_or("Ошибка в коде инъекции профиля")?;
        let original = self.read_bytes(site, *size).ok_or("Не удалось прочитать код игры")?;
        let cave = match self.caves.get(&def.id) {
            Some(&c) => c,
            None => {
                let c = self
                    .proc
                    .alloc_near(site, inject::CAVE_SIZE)
                    .ok_or("Не удалось выделить память рядом с кодом игры")?;
                self.caves.insert(def.id.clone(), c);
                c
            }
        };
        let cave_code = inject::assemble(&parts, site, *size, &original, cave)?;
        if let Some(p) = param {
            self.proc.write(cave + inject::PARAM_OFFSET, p);
        }
        if !self.proc.write(cave, &cave_code) {
            return Err("Не удалось записать код инъекции".into());
        }
        let jump = inject::site_jump(site, *size, cave)?;
        if !self.proc.write(site, &jump) {
            return Err("Не удалось изменить код игры".into());
        }
        self.active
            .insert(def.id.clone(), Active::Inject { site, original, cave });
        Ok(())
    }

    /// Switches any active effect of a cheat off, restoring patched code.
    pub(super) fn effect_off(&mut self, id: &str) -> bool {
        match self.active.remove(id) {
            Some(Active::Patch { addr, original }) => {
                self.proc.write(addr, &original);
                true
            }
            Some(Active::Inject { site, original, .. }) => {
                self.proc.write(site, &original);
                true
            }
            Some(Active::Freeze(_)) => true,
            None => false,
        }
    }

    pub(super) fn chain_count(&self, cheat: &str) -> usize {
        self.chains.get(cheat).map_or(0, Vec::len)
    }

    pub(super) fn def(&self, id: &str) -> Option<&CheatDef> {
        self.profile.cheats.iter().find(|c| c.id == id)
    }

    pub(super) fn rebuild_chains(&mut self) {
        self.chains.clear();
        self.pick.clear();
        for c in &self.profile.cheats {
            let list: Vec<Chain> = match self.calib.cheats.get(&c.id) {
                Some(e) if !e.chains.is_empty() => e.chains.clone(),
                _ => self
                    .profile
                    .builds
                    .get(&self.build)
                    .and_then(|b| b.get(&c.id))
                    .cloned()
                    .unwrap_or_default(),
            };
            let parsed: Vec<Parsed> = list
                .iter()
                .filter_map(|ch| ch.parse(&self.modules))
                .collect();
            if !parsed.is_empty() {
                self.chains.insert(c.id.clone(), parsed);
            }
        }
    }

    pub(super) fn vtype(&self, def: &CheatDef) -> VType {
        self.calib
            .cheats
            .get(&def.id)
            .map(|e| e.vtype)
            .unwrap_or(def.vtype)
    }

    pub(super) fn read_bytes(&self, addr: usize, len: usize) -> Option<Vec<u8>> {
        let mut b = vec![0u8; len];
        self.proc.read(addr, &mut b).then_some(b)
    }

    fn aob_site(
        &mut self,
        key: &str,
        module: Option<&str>,
        pattern: &str,
    ) -> Result<usize, Unresolved> {
        if let Some(v) = self.aob_cache.get(key) {
            return v.ok_or(Unresolved::Missing);
        }
        let m = match module {
            Some(n) if !n.trim().is_empty() => self
                .modules
                .iter()
                .find(|m| m.name.eq_ignore_ascii_case(n.trim()))
                .cloned(),
            _ => Some(self.main.clone()),
        };
        let m = m.ok_or(Unresolved::Waiting)?;
        let pat = aob::parse(pattern).ok_or(Unresolved::Invalid)?;
        let site = aob::find(&self.proc, &m, &pat);
        self.aob_cache.insert(key.to_string(), site);
        site.ok_or(Unresolved::Missing)
    }

    /// Address of the cheat's value (or code patch site) in this session.
    pub(super) fn resolve(&mut self, id: &str) -> Result<usize, Unresolved> {
        let loc = self.def(id).ok_or(Unresolved::Invalid)?.locator.clone();
        match loc {
            Locator::Pointer { module, offsets } => {
                pointer::resolve_chain(&self.proc, &self.modules, module.as_deref(), &offsets)
                    .ok_or(Unresolved::Waiting)
            }
            Locator::Aob {
                module,
                pattern,
                rip,
                add,
                offsets,
            } => {
                let site = self.aob_site(id, module.as_deref(), &pattern)?;
                let mut addr = site as i64;
                if let Some([disp, len]) = rip {
                    let d = self.proc.read_u32(site + disp).ok_or(Unresolved::Waiting)? as i32;
                    addr = site as i64 + len as i64 + d as i64;
                }
                let mut addr = (addr + add) as usize;
                for o in &offsets {
                    let off = pointer::parse_hex(o).ok_or(Unresolved::Invalid)?;
                    addr = (self.proc.read_ptr(addr).ok_or(Unresolved::Waiting)? as i64 + off)
                        as usize;
                }
                Ok(addr)
            }
            Locator::Patch {
                module,
                pattern,
                offset,
                ..
            } => Ok((self.aob_site(id, module.as_deref(), &pattern)? as i64 + offset) as usize),
            Locator::Inject {
                module,
                pattern,
                offset,
                ..
            } => Ok((self.aob_site(id, module.as_deref(), &pattern)? as i64 + offset) as usize),
            Locator::Calibrate { .. } => {
                if self.chains.contains_key(id) {
                    if let Ok(a) = self.pick_chain(id) {
                        return Ok(a);
                    }
                }
                if let Some(&a) = self.session_addresses.get(id) {
                    return Ok(a);
                }
                if self.chains.contains_key(id) {
                    Err(Unresolved::Waiting)
                } else {
                    Err(Unresolved::NeedsCalibration)
                }
            }
        }
    }

    /// Chains are stored best first (shortest, smallest offsets; confirmed ones survive
    /// re-calibration), so the first chain that leads to readable memory wins.
    /// Majority votes are not used: many long chains can agree on the same wrong address.
    fn pick_chain(&mut self, id: &str) -> Result<usize, Unresolved> {
        let chains = self.chains.get(id).ok_or(Unresolved::NeedsCalibration)?;
        if let Some(&(i, t)) = self.pick.get(id) {
            if t.elapsed() < Duration::from_secs(3) {
                if let Some(a) = chains.get(i).and_then(|c| c.resolve(&self.proc)) {
                    return Ok(a);
                }
            }
        }
        let mut probe = [0u8; 4];
        for (i, c) in chains.iter().enumerate() {
            if let Some(a) = c
                .resolve(&self.proc)
                .filter(|&a| self.proc.read(a, &mut probe))
            {
                self.pick.insert(id.to_string(), (i, Instant::now()));
                return Ok(a);
            }
        }
        Err(Unresolved::Waiting)
    }
}
```

`src/app/settings.rs`:

```rs
//! Configuration, per-cheat preferences and settings persistence.

use std::collections::{HashMap, HashSet};
use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};

use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};

use crate::profile::{self, CheatDef};

use super::App;

#[derive(Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(default)]
pub(crate) struct Settings {
    pub(crate) token: String,
    pub(crate) port: u16,
    pub(crate) auto_attach: bool,
    pub(crate) hotkeys: bool,
    /// Phone remote over the LAN (off by default: only this PC can open the UI).
    pub(crate) remote: bool,
    pub(crate) sounds: bool,
    pub(crate) fullscreen: bool,
    /// "game/cheat" -> user's value and hotkey.
    overrides: HashMap<String, Override>,
    /// "game/cheat" pinned to the top of the cheat list.
    pins: HashSet<String>,
}

impl Default for Settings {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Settings {
            token: gen_token(),
            port: 7777,
            auto_attach: true,
            hotkeys: true,
            remote: false,
            sounds: true,
            fullscreen: false,
            overrides: HashMap::new(),
            pins: HashSet::new(),
        }
    }
}

/// Address of this PC in the local network (no packets are sent; the socket only picks a route).
pub(crate) fn detect_lan_ip() -> String {
    std::net::UdpSocket::bind("0.0.0.0:0")
        .and_then(|s| {
            s.connect("192.168.0.1:9")?;
            s.local_addr()
        })
        .map(|a| a.ip().to_string())
        .unwrap_or_else(|_| "127.0.0.1".into())
}

#[derive(Clone, Default, Serialize, Deserialize)]
struct Override {
    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
    value: Option<String>,
    /// `Some("")` disables the profile's hotkey.
    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
    hotkey: Option<String>,
    /// Second hotkey ("decrease"); `Some("")` disables the profile's one.
    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
    hotkey_down: Option<String>,
}

pub(crate) fn gen_token() -> String {
    use std::hash::{BuildHasher, Hasher};
    let nanos = std::time::SystemTime::now()
        .duration_since(std::time::UNIX_EPOCH)
        .map(|d| d.as_nanos())
        .unwrap_or(0);
    let mut out = String::new();
    for salt in 0..2u32 {
        let mut h = std::collections::hash_map::RandomState::new().build_hasher();
        h.write_u128(nanos);
        h.write_u32(std::process::id() ^ salt);
        out.push_str(&format!("{:016x}", h.finish()));
    }
    out[..20].to_string()
}

pub(super) fn data_dir() -> PathBuf {
    if let Some(d) = std::env::var_os("FAHRENHACK_DATA") {
        return PathBuf::from(d);
    }
    PathBuf::from(std::env::var_os("APPDATA").unwrap_or_else(|| ".".into())).join("FahrenHack")
}

impl Settings {
    pub(super) fn load(data_dir: &Path) -> Self {
        let mut settings: Self =
            profile::read_json(&data_dir.join("settings.json")).unwrap_or_default();
        if settings.token.len() < 12 {
            settings.token = gen_token();
        }
        settings
    }

    fn override_key(game: &str, cheat: &str) -> String {
        format!("{game}/{cheat}")
    }

    /// The user's value, else the profile's. `None` = freeze the current value.
    pub(super) fn cheat_value(&self, game: &str, def: &CheatDef) -> Option<String> {
        let o = self.overrides.get(&Self::override_key(game, &def.id));
        o.and_then(|o| o.value.clone())
            .or_else(|| def.value.clone())
            .filter(|s| !s.trim().is_empty() && !s.trim().eq_ignore_ascii_case("current"))
    }

    pub(super) fn cheat_hotkey(&self, game: &str, def: &CheatDef) -> Option<String> {
        match self
            .overrides
            .get(&Self::override_key(game, &def.id))
            .and_then(|o| o.hotkey.clone())
        {
            Some(h) => (!h.trim().is_empty()).then_some(h),
            None => def.hotkey.clone(),
        }
    }

    pub(super) fn cheat_hotkey_down(&self, game: &str, def: &CheatDef) -> Option<String> {
        match self
            .overrides
            .get(&Self::override_key(game, &def.id))
            .and_then(|o| o.hotkey_down.clone())
        {
            Some(h) => (!h.trim().is_empty()).then_some(h),
            None => def.hotkey_down.clone(),
        }
    }

    pub(super) fn set_cheat_override(
        &mut self,
        game: &str,
        cheat: &str,
        value: String,
        hotkey: String,
        hotkey_down: Option<String>,
    ) {
        let entry = self
            .overrides
            .entry(Self::override_key(game, cheat))
            .or_default();
        entry.value = (!value.is_empty()).then_some(value);
        entry.hotkey = Some(hotkey);
        if hotkey_down.is_some() {
            entry.hotkey_down = hotkey_down;
        }
    }

    /// Remembers a slider / select position without touching the hotkeys.
    pub(super) fn remember_value(&mut self, game: &str, cheat: &str, value: String) {
        self.overrides
            .entry(Self::override_key(game, cheat))
            .or_default()
            .value = Some(value);
    }

    pub(super) fn is_pinned(&self, game: &str, cheat: &str) -> bool {
        self.pins.contains(&Self::override_key(game, cheat))
    }

    /// Returns whether the cheat is pinned now.
    pub(super) fn toggle_pin(&mut self, game: &str, cheat: &str) -> bool {
        let key = Self::override_key(game, cheat);
        if self.pins.remove(&key) {
            false
        } else {
            self.pins.insert(key);
            true
        }
    }

    pub(super) fn clear_cheat_override(&mut self, game: &str, cheat: &str) {
        self.overrides.remove(&Self::override_key(game, cheat));
    }
}

impl App {
    pub(crate) fn save_settings(&self) {
        if let Ok(s) = serde_json::to_string_pretty(&self.settings) {
            let _ = std::fs::write(self.data_dir.join("settings.json"), s);
        }
    }

    pub(crate) fn lan_url(&self) -> String {
        format!(
            "http://{}:{}/?t={}",
            self.lan_ip, self.port, self.settings.token
        )
    }
}
```

`src/app/view.rs`:

```rs
//! JSON projections shared by the desktop UI and the phone remote.

use std::collections::HashSet;

use serde_json::{json, Value};

use crate::mem;
use crate::profile::{self, CheatDef, GameProfile, Kind, Locator};
use crate::steam;

use super::settings::Settings;
use super::App;

impl App {
    pub(crate) fn state_json(&mut self) -> Value {
        let settings = json!({
            "auto_attach": self.settings.auto_attach,
            "hotkeys": self.settings.hotkeys,
            "remote": self.settings.remote,
            "sounds": self.settings.sounds,
            "fullscreen": self.settings.fullscreen,
            "data_dir": self.data_dir.display().to_string(),
        });
        let mut out = json!({
            "version": env!("CARGO_PKG_VERSION"),
            "lan_url": self.lan_url(),
            "settings": settings,
            "scan": self.scan.as_ref().map(|s| s.json()),
            "job": self.job.clone(),
            "attached": null,
            "cheats": [],
        });
        let Some(g) = self.game.as_ref() else {
            return out;
        };
        let gid = g.profile.id.clone();
        let defs = g.profile.cheats.clone();
        let extra: Vec<Value> = defs
            .iter()
            .map(|d| cheat_static(d, &self.settings, &gid))
            .collect();
        let categories = g.profile.categories.clone();
        let g = self.game.as_mut().unwrap();
        out["attached"] = json!({
            "pid": g.proc.pid,
            "exe": g.proc.name,
            "game": gid,
            "title": g.profile.title,
            "known": g.known,
            "build": g.build,
            "x86": g.proc.is32,
        });
        let cheats: Vec<Value> = defs
            .iter()
            .zip(extra)
            .map(|(d, mut item)| {
                let r = g.resolve(&d.id);
                let vt = g.vtype(d);
                let patch = matches!(d.locator, Locator::Patch { .. } | Locator::Inject { .. });
                let current = match r {
                    Ok(a) if !patch => g.read_bytes(a, vt.size()).map(|b| vt.fmt(&b)),
                    _ => None,
                };
                let calibration = match &d.locator {
                    Locator::Calibrate {
                        mode,
                        prompt,
                        steps,
                    } => {
                        let e = g.calib.cheats.get(&d.id);
                        json!({
                            "mode": mode,
                            "prompt": prompt,
                            "steps": steps,
                            "chains": g.chain_count(&d.id),
                            "confirmations": e.map(|e| e.confirmations).unwrap_or(0),
                            "session": g.session_addresses.contains_key(&d.id),
                        })
                    }
                    _ => Value::Null,
                };
                item["vtype"] = json!(vt);
                item["status"] = json!(match r { Ok(_) => "ready", Err(e) => e.code() });
                item["active"] = json!(g.active.contains_key(&d.id));
                item["current"] = json!(current);
                item["addr"] = json!(r.ok().map(|a| format!("{a:X}")));
                item["calibration"] = calibration;
                item
            })
            .collect();
        out["cheats"] = Value::Array(cheats);
        out["categories"] = json!(categories);
        out
    }

    pub(crate) fn games_json(&self) -> Value {
        let procs: HashSet<String> = mem::list_processes()
            .into_iter()
            .map(|p| p.name.to_lowercase())
            .collect();
        let attached = self.game.as_ref().map(|g| g.profile.id.clone());
        let mut list: Vec<Value> = self
            .profiles
            .iter()
            .map(|g| self.game_json(g, true, &procs, attached.as_deref()))
            .collect();
        if let Some(g) = self.game.as_ref().filter(|g| !g.known) {
            list.push(self.game_json(&g.profile, false, &procs, attached.as_deref()));
        }
        Value::Array(list)
    }

    fn game_json(
        &self,
        g: &GameProfile,
        known: bool,
        procs: &HashSet<String>,
        attached: Option<&str>,
    ) -> Value {
        let appid = g.steam_appid;
        let art = |k: &str| appid.and_then(|a| steam::art(a, k)).is_some();
        let mut cheats = g.cheats.clone();
        if known && attached != Some(g.id.as_str()) {
            cheats.extend(profile::load_custom(&self.data_dir, &g.id));
        }
        json!({
            "id": g.id,
            "title": g.title,
            "subtitle": g.subtitle,
            "accent": g.accent,
            "notes": g.notes,
            "known": known,
            "steam_appid": appid,
            "installed": appid.is_some_and(|a| steam::install_dir(a).is_some()),
            "running": g.processes.iter().any(|p| procs.contains(&p.to_lowercase())),
            "attached": attached == Some(g.id.as_str()),
            "art": { "cover": art("cover"), "hero": art("hero"), "logo": art("logo") },
            "categories": g.categories,
            "cheats": cheats.iter().map(|c| cheat_static(c, &self.settings, &g.id)).collect::<Vec<_>>(),
        })
    }
}

/// Everything about a cheat that does not need the running game.
fn cheat_static(c: &CheatDef, settings: &Settings, game: &str) -> Value {
    json!({
        "id": c.id,
        "name": c.name,
        "category": c.category,
        "description": c.description,
        "icon": c.icon,
        "kind": cheat_kind(c),
        "vtype": c.vtype,
        "value": settings.cheat_value(game, c),
        "hotkey": settings.cheat_hotkey(game, c),
        "hotkey_down": settings.cheat_hotkey_down(game, c),
        "info": c.info,
        "warn": c.warn,
        "step": c.step(),
        "min": c.min,
        "max": c.max,
        "off_value": c.off_value,
        "options": c.options,
        "pinned": settings.is_pinned(game, &c.id),
        "experimental": c.experimental,
        "custom": c.custom,
    })
}

fn cheat_kind(definition: &CheatDef) -> &'static str {
    match definition.kind {
        Kind::Number => "number",
        Kind::Slider => "slider",
        Kind::Select => "select",
        Kind::Set => "set",
        Kind::Freeze if matches!(definition.locator, Locator::Patch { .. } | Locator::Inject { .. }) => "patch",
        Kind::Freeze => "freeze",
    }
}
```

`src/app/workers.rs`:

```rs
//! Periodic freezing, hotkey polling and automatic game attachment.

use std::collections::HashMap;
use std::thread;
use std::time::Duration;

use crate::hotkey::Hotkey;
use crate::mem;

use super::{lock, Shared};

pub(crate) fn spawn_workers(app: &Shared) {
    let a = app.clone();
    thread::spawn(move || loop {
        thread::sleep(Duration::from_millis(40));
        lock(&a).tick();
    });

    let a = app.clone();
    thread::spawn(move || hotkey_loop(a));

    let a = app.clone();
    thread::spawn(move || auto_attach_loop(a));
}

fn hotkey_loop(app: Shared) {
    let mut prev: HashMap<String, bool> = HashMap::new();
    loop {
        thread::sleep(Duration::from_millis(30));
        let mut a = lock(&app);
        if !a.settings.hotkeys {
            continue;
        }
        let Some(g) = a.game.as_ref() else { continue };
        let gid = g.profile.id.clone();
        // (cheat id, name, key, +1 = toggle / increase, -1 = decrease)
        let mut keys: Vec<(String, String, Hotkey, i32)> = Vec::new();
        for c in &g.profile.cheats {
            let up = a.settings.cheat_hotkey(&gid, c).and_then(|h| Hotkey::parse(&h));
            let down = a.settings.cheat_hotkey_down(&gid, c).and_then(|h| Hotkey::parse(&h));
            if let Some(hk) = up {
                keys.push((c.id.clone(), c.name.clone(), hk, 1));
            }
            if let Some(hk) = down {
                keys.push((c.id.clone(), c.name.clone(), hk, -1));
            }
        }
        for (id, name, hk, dir) in keys {
            let down = hk.pressed();
            let was = prev.insert(format!("{id}\u{1}{dir}"), down).unwrap_or(false);
            if down && !was {
                match a.step(&id, dir) {
                    Ok(on) => println!("[hotkey] {name}{}", if on { ": ВКЛ" } else { "" }),
                    Err(e) => println!("[hotkey] {name}: {e}"),
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

/// Attach by itself when a game with a profile is running.
fn auto_attach_loop(app: Shared) {
    loop {
        thread::sleep(Duration::from_secs(2));
        let (wanted, skip) = {
            let a = lock(&app);
            if a.game.is_some() || !a.settings.auto_attach {
                continue;
            }
            let w: Vec<String> = a
                .profiles
                .iter()
                .flat_map(|p| p.processes.iter().map(|s| s.to_lowercase()))
                .collect();
            (w, a.skip_pid)
        };
        if wanted.is_empty() {
            continue;
        }
        let Some(p) = mem::list_processes()
            .into_iter()
            .find(|p| wanted.contains(&p.name.to_lowercase()) && Some(p.pid) != skip)
        else {
            continue;
        };
        let mut a = lock(&app);
        if a.game.is_some() {
            continue;
        }
        let n = a.attach_failures.get(&p.pid).copied().unwrap_or(0);
        if n >= 5 {
            continue;
        }
        if let Err(e) = a.attach(p.pid, &p.name) {
            println!("[!] Автоподключение к {}: {e}", p.name);
            a.attach_failures.insert(p.pid, n + 1);
        }
    }
}
```

`src/hotkey.rs`:

```rs
//! Global hotkeys polled with GetAsyncKeyState (work while the game has focus).

use windows_sys::Win32::UI::Input::KeyboardAndMouse::GetAsyncKeyState;

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct Hotkey {
    vk: i32,
    ctrl: bool,
    shift: bool,
    alt: bool,
}

fn down(vk: i32) -> bool {
    unsafe { (GetAsyncKeyState(vk) as u16) & 0x8000 != 0 }
}

impl Hotkey {
    /// "F1", "CTRL+F5", "SHIFT+NUM3", "ALT+K" …
    pub fn parse(s: &str) -> Option<Hotkey> {
        let mut hk = Hotkey { vk: 0, ctrl: false, shift: false, alt: false };
        for part in s.to_uppercase().split('+').map(str::trim) {
            match part {
                "CTRL" | "CONTROL" => hk.ctrl = true,
                "SHIFT" => hk.shift = true,
                "ALT" => hk.alt = true,
                "" => {}
                p => {
                    if hk.vk != 0 {
                        return None;
                    }
                    hk.vk = if let Some(n) = p.strip_prefix('F').and_then(|n| n.parse::<i32>().ok()) {
                        if !(1..=24).contains(&n) {
                            return None;
                        }
                        0x6F + n
                    } else if let Some(n) = p.strip_prefix("NUM").and_then(|n| n.parse::<i32>().ok()) {
                        if !(0..=9).contains(&n) {
                            return None;
                        }
                        0x60 + n
                    } else if p.len() == 1 && p.chars().all(|c| c.is_ascii_alphanumeric()) {
                        p.as_bytes()[0] as i32
                    } else {
                        return None;
                    };
                }
            }
        }
        (hk.vk != 0).then_some(hk)
    }

    pub fn pressed(&self) -> bool {
        down(self.vk) && down(0x11) == self.ctrl && down(0x10) == self.shift && down(0x12) == self.alt
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::Hotkey;

    #[test]
    fn parse_keys() {
        assert!(Hotkey::parse("F1").is_some());
        assert!(Hotkey::parse("ctrl+F12").is_some());
        assert!(Hotkey::parse("SHIFT+NUM5").is_some());
        assert!(Hotkey::parse("ALT+K").is_some());
        assert!(Hotkey::parse("F25").is_none());
        assert!(Hotkey::parse("F1+F2").is_none());
        assert!(Hotkey::parse("CTRL").is_none());
    }
}

```

`src/inject.rs`:

```rs
//! Code injection through a code cave.
//!
//! `size` bytes at the patch site are replaced by `jmp cave` (E9 rel32) plus NOPs. The cave is
//! allocated within ±2 GB of the site and holds the profile's code; the cheat's parameter
//! (for sliders, e.g. a multiplier) lives in a data slot at `cave + PARAM_OFFSET`.
//!
//! The `code` template is hex bytes separated by spaces, plus placeholders:
//! - `{orig}`      the original bytes overwritten at the site (must not be RIP-relative);
//! - `{return}`    `jmp` back to the first instruction after the overwritten bytes (5 bytes);
//! - `{param}`     rel32 displacement to the parameter slot, for an instruction whose
//!                 displacement is its last field, e.g. `F3 0F 59 05 {param}` = `mulss xmm0,[param]`;
//! - `{param_abs}` 8-byte absolute address of the parameter, e.g. `48 B8 {param_abs}`.

pub const CAVE_SIZE: usize = 0x1000;
pub const PARAM_OFFSET: usize = 0xF00;

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum Part {
    Bytes(Vec<u8>),
    Orig,
    Return,
    Param,
    ParamAbs,
}

pub fn parse(code: &str) -> Option<Vec<Part>> {
    let mut parts = Vec::new();
    for token in code.split_whitespace() {
        let part = match token {
            "{orig}" => Part::Orig,
            "{return}" => Part::Return,
            "{param}" => Part::Param,
            "{param_abs}" => Part::ParamAbs,
            hex => Part::Bytes(vec![u8::from_str_radix(hex, 16).ok().filter(|_| hex.len() == 2)?]),
        };
        match (parts.last_mut(), part) {
            (Some(Part::Bytes(prev)), Part::Bytes(b)) => prev.extend(b),
            (_, p) => parts.push(p),
        }
    }
    (!parts.is_empty()).then_some(parts)
}

fn rel32(from_end: usize, to: usize) -> Result<[u8; 4], String> {
    let d = to as i64 - from_end as i64;
    i32::try_from(d)
        .map(|v| v.to_le_bytes())
        .map_err(|_| "Код-пещера слишком далеко от кода игры".to_string())
}

/// Machine code for the cave.
pub fn assemble(parts: &[Part], site: usize, size: usize, original: &[u8], cave: usize) -> Result<Vec<u8>, String> {
    let param = cave + PARAM_OFFSET;
    let mut out = Vec::new();
    for p in parts {
        match p {
            Part::Bytes(b) => out.extend_from_slice(b),
            Part::Orig => out.extend_from_slice(original),
            Part::Return => {
                out.push(0xE9);
                let end = cave + out.len() + 4;
                out.extend_from_slice(&rel32(end, site + size)?);
            }
            Part::Param => {
                let end = cave + out.len() + 4;
                out.extend_from_slice(&rel32(end, param)?);
            }
            Part::ParamAbs => out.extend_from_slice(&(param as u64).to_le_bytes()),
        }
    }
    if out.len() > PARAM_OFFSET {
        return Err("Код инъекции не помещается в код-пещеру".into());
    }
    Ok(out)
}

/// Bytes written over the site: `jmp cave` padded with NOPs.
pub fn site_jump(site: usize, size: usize, cave: usize) -> Result<Vec<u8>, String> {
    if size < 5 {
        return Err("Для перехода нужно минимум 5 байт".into());
    }
    let mut out = vec![0xE9];
    out.extend_from_slice(&rel32(site + 5, cave)?);
    out.resize(size, 0x90);
    Ok(out)
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn parse_template() {
        let p = parse("8B 15 {param} F0 29 51 18 {orig} {return}").unwrap();
        assert_eq!(
            p,
            vec![Part::Bytes(vec![0x8B, 0x15]), Part::Param, Part::Bytes(vec![0xF0, 0x29, 0x51, 0x18]), Part::Orig, Part::Return]
        );
        assert!(parse("ZZ").is_none());
        assert!(parse("123").is_none());
        assert!(parse("").is_none());
    }

    #[test]
    fn assemble_offsets() {
        let site = 0x1_4000_1000;
        let cave = 0x1_4100_0000;
        let code = assemble(&parse("8B 15 {param} {return}").unwrap(), site, 5, &[0x90; 5], cave).unwrap();
        // mov edx,[rip+d]: d = param - (cave + 6)
        let d = i32::from_le_bytes(code[2..6].try_into().unwrap()) as i64;
        assert_eq!(cave as i64 + 6 + d, (cave + PARAM_OFFSET) as i64);
        // jmp back: target = cave + 11 + rel
        assert_eq!(code[6], 0xE9);
        let r = i32::from_le_bytes(code[7..11].try_into().unwrap()) as i64;
        assert_eq!(cave as i64 + 11 + r, (site + 5) as i64);
        let j = site_jump(site, 7, cave).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(j.len(), 7);
        assert_eq!(&j[5..], &[0x90, 0x90]);
        let r = i32::from_le_bytes(j[1..5].try_into().unwrap()) as i64;
        assert_eq!(site as i64 + 5 + r, cave as i64);
        assert!(site_jump(site, 4, cave).is_err());
        assert!(site_jump(site, 5, site + (3 << 30)).is_err());
    }
}

```

`src/logo.rs`:

```rs
// FahrenHack logo rasterizer, shared by build.rs (exe icon) and the app (window icon).
// The geometry is a 1024×1024 box and matches assets/logo.svg:
// a navy rounded square, a white "F" whose bars tuck under a teal "H" and a teal dot.
// This file is `include!`d by build.rs, so it must not use crate paths.

#[allow(dead_code)]
const NAVY: [f32; 3] = [18.0, 35.0, 58.0];
#[allow(dead_code)]
const TEAL: [f32; 3] = [75.0, 192.0, 192.0];
#[allow(dead_code)]
const WHITE: [f32; 3] = [255.0, 255.0, 255.0];

#[allow(dead_code)]
fn sd_box(px: f32, py: f32, x0: f32, y0: f32, x1: f32, y1: f32, r: f32) -> f32 {
    let (cx, cy) = ((x0 + x1) * 0.5, (y0 + y1) * 0.5);
    let (hx, hy) = ((x1 - x0) * 0.5 - r, (y1 - y0) * 0.5 - r);
    let (qx, qy) = ((px - cx).abs() - hx, (py - cy).abs() - hy);
    let (ox, oy) = (qx.max(0.0), qy.max(0.0));
    (ox * ox + oy * oy).sqrt() + qx.max(qy).min(0.0) - r
}

#[allow(dead_code)]
fn sd_capsule(px: f32, py: f32, ax: f32, ay: f32, bx: f32, by: f32, r: f32) -> f32 {
    let (pax, pay, bax, bay) = (px - ax, py - ay, bx - ax, by - ay);
    let h = ((pax * bax + pay * bay) / (bax * bax + bay * bay)).clamp(0.0, 1.0);
    let (dx, dy) = (pax - bax * h, pay - bay * h);
    (dx * dx + dy * dy).sqrt() - r
}

#[allow(dead_code)]
fn sd_circle(px: f32, py: f32, cx: f32, cy: f32, r: f32) -> f32 {
    ((px - cx).powi(2) + (py - cy).powi(2)).sqrt() - r
}

/// Signed distances to the background, the white strokes and the teal strokes.
#[allow(dead_code)]
fn logo_sdf(x: f32, y: f32) -> (f32, f32, f32) {
    let bg = sd_box(x, y, 0.0, 0.0, 1024.0, 1024.0, 200.0);
    let white = sd_capsule(x, y, 240.0, 280.0, 240.0, 752.0, 48.0)
        .min(sd_box(x, y, 240.0, 232.0, 576.0, 328.0, 0.0))
        .min(sd_box(x, y, 240.0, 456.0, 440.0, 552.0, 0.0));
    let teal = sd_circle(x, y, 440.0, 504.0, 48.0)
        .min(sd_capsule(x, y, 576.0, 280.0, 576.0, 752.0, 48.0))
        .min(sd_capsule(x, y, 800.0, 280.0, 800.0, 752.0, 48.0))
        .min(sd_box(x, y, 576.0, 456.0, 800.0, 552.0, 0.0));
    (bg, white, teal)
}

/// RGBA pixels of the logo, `size`×`size`, anti-aliased.
#[allow(dead_code)]
pub fn logo_rgba(size: u32) -> Vec<u8> {
    let s = size as f32 / 1024.0;
    let mut out = Vec::with_capacity((size * size * 4) as usize);
    for py in 0..size {
        for px in 0..size {
            let (x, y) = ((px as f32 + 0.5) / s, (py as f32 + 0.5) / s);
            let (bg, w, t) = logo_sdf(x, y);
            let cov = |d: f32| (0.5 - d * s).clamp(0.0, 1.0);
            let (a, cw, ct) = (cov(bg), cov(w), cov(t));
            let mut c = NAVY;
            for i in 0..3 {
                c[i] += (WHITE[i] - c[i]) * cw;
                c[i] += (TEAL[i] - c[i]) * ct;
            }
            out.extend_from_slice(&[c[0].round() as u8, c[1].round() as u8, c[2].round() as u8, (a * 255.0).round() as u8]);
        }
    }
    out
}

```

`src/main.rs`:

```rs
// No console window in release builds; `--headless` / `--browser` attach one.
#![cfg_attr(not(debug_assertions), windows_subsystem = "windows")]

//! FahrenHack: open-source trainer for single-player PC games.
//! A console-style desktop UI, ready game profiles and a phone remote over the LAN.

mod aob;
mod app;
mod hotkey;
mod inject;
mod logo;
mod mem;
mod pointer;
mod profile;
mod scan;
mod steam;
mod web;
mod window;

use std::sync::{Arc, Mutex};

use windows_sys::Win32::System::Console::{AllocConsole, AttachConsole, ATTACH_PARENT_PROCESS};
use windows_sys::Win32::UI::WindowsAndMessaging::{MessageBoxW, MB_ICONERROR, MB_OK};

const HELP: &str = "FahrenHack — трейнер для одиночных игр

  fahrenhack              окно приложения
  fahrenhack --browser    открыть интерфейс в браузере вместо окна
  fahrenhack --headless   только сервер для телефона (ссылка и QR в консоли)

Данные: %APPDATA%\\FahrenHack (можно переопределить переменной FAHRENHACK_DATA)";

fn wide(s: &str) -> Vec<u16> {
    s.encode_utf16().chain(std::iter::once(0)).collect()
}

fn fatal(msg: &str) -> ! {
    eprintln!("[x] {msg}");
    let (text, title) = (wide(msg), wide("FahrenHack"));
    unsafe {
        MessageBoxW(std::ptr::null_mut(), text.as_ptr(), title.as_ptr(), MB_OK | MB_ICONERROR);
    }
    std::process::exit(1);
}

fn console() {
    unsafe {
        if AttachConsole(ATTACH_PARENT_PROCESS) == 0 {
            AllocConsole();
        }
    }
}

fn banner(local: &str, lan: Option<&str>, data: &str) {
    println!("=== FahrenHack {} ===", env!("CARGO_PKG_VERSION"));
    println!("ПК:      {local}");
    println!("Данные:  {data}");
    let Some(lan) = lan else {
        println!("Пульт для телефона выключен: включите его в разделе «Пульт».\n");
        return;
    };
    println!("Телефон: {lan}");
    println!("(телефон должен быть в той же сети Wi-Fi: отсканируйте QR-код)\n");
    if let Ok(code) = qrcode::QrCode::new(lan.as_bytes()) {
        use qrcode::render::unicode::Dense1x2;
        let s = code.render::<Dense1x2>().dark_color(Dense1x2::Light).light_color(Dense1x2::Dark).build();
        println!("{s}\n");
    }
}

fn main() {
    let args: Vec<String> = std::env::args().skip(1).collect();
    let flag = |f: &str| args.iter().any(|a| a == f);
    if flag("--help") || flag("-h") {
        console();
        println!("{HELP}");
        return;
    }
    let headless = flag("--headless");
    let browser = flag("--browser");
    if headless || browser {
        console();
    }

    let mut state = app::App::new();
    state.lan_ip = app::detect_lan_ip();
    let app: app::Shared = Arc::new(Mutex::new(state));
    app::spawn_workers(&app);

    let (tx, rx) = std::sync::mpsc::channel();
    {
        let app = app.clone();
        std::thread::spawn(move || {
            match tokio::runtime::Builder::new_multi_thread().worker_threads(2).enable_all().build() {
                Ok(rt) => rt.block_on(web::serve(app, tx)),
                Err(e) => {
                    let _ = tx.send(Err(e.to_string()));
                }
            }
        });
    }
    let port = match rx.recv() {
        Ok(Ok(p)) => p,
        Ok(Err(e)) => fatal(&format!("Не удалось запустить сервер: {e}")),
        Err(_) => fatal("Не удалось запустить сервер"),
    };

    let (local, lan, data_dir, fullscreen) = {
        let mut a = app::lock(&app);
        a.port = port;
        (
            format!("http://127.0.0.1:{port}/?t={}", a.settings.token),
            a.settings.remote.then(|| a.lan_url()),
            a.data_dir.clone(),
            a.settings.fullscreen,
        )
    };
    banner(&local, lan.as_deref(), &data_dir.display().to_string());

    if headless {
        loop {
            std::thread::park();
        }
    }
    if browser {
        let _ = steam::open(&local);
        loop {
            std::thread::park();
        }
    }

    let exit_app = app.clone();
    let on_exit: Box<dyn FnOnce()> = Box::new(move || app::lock(&exit_app).detach());
    if let Err(e) = window::run(format!("{local}&desktop=1"), data_dir, fullscreen, on_exit) {
        eprintln!("[!] Окно не открылось ({e}), открываю интерфейс в браузере");
        let _ = steam::open(&local);
        loop {
            std::thread::park();
        }
    }
}
```

`src/mem.rs`:

```rs
//! Access to another process's memory through WinAPI.

use std::ffi::c_void;
use std::mem::{size_of, zeroed};

use windows_sys::Win32::Foundation::{CloseHandle, GetLastError, HANDLE, INVALID_HANDLE_VALUE};
use windows_sys::Win32::System::Diagnostics::Debug::{ReadProcessMemory, WriteProcessMemory};
use windows_sys::Win32::System::Diagnostics::ToolHelp::{
    CreateToolhelp32Snapshot, Module32FirstW, Module32NextW, Process32FirstW, Process32NextW, Thread32First,
    Thread32Next, MODULEENTRY32W, PROCESSENTRY32W, TH32CS_SNAPMODULE, TH32CS_SNAPMODULE32, TH32CS_SNAPPROCESS,
    TH32CS_SNAPTHREAD, THREADENTRY32,
};
use windows_sys::Win32::System::Memory::{
    VirtualAllocEx, VirtualProtectEx, VirtualQueryEx, MEMORY_BASIC_INFORMATION, MEM_COMMIT, MEM_FREE, MEM_MAPPED,
    MEM_RESERVE,
    PAGE_EXECUTE_READ, PAGE_EXECUTE_READWRITE, PAGE_EXECUTE_WRITECOPY, PAGE_GUARD, PAGE_READONLY,
    PAGE_READWRITE, PAGE_WRITECOPY,
};
use windows_sys::Win32::System::Threading::{
    GetExitCodeProcess, IsWow64Process, OpenProcess, OpenThread, PROCESS_QUERY_INFORMATION,
    PROCESS_VM_OPERATION, PROCESS_VM_READ, PROCESS_VM_WRITE, THREAD_QUERY_INFORMATION,
};

const STILL_ACTIVE: u32 = 259;

#[repr(C)]
struct ThreadBasicInformation {
    exit_status: i32,
    teb: *mut c_void,
    client_id: [usize; 2],
    affinity_mask: usize,
    priority: i32,
    base_priority: i32,
}

#[link(name = "ntdll")]
extern "system" {
    fn NtQueryInformationThread(thread: HANDLE, class: u32, info: *mut c_void, len: u32, ret: *mut u32) -> i32;
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, serde::Serialize)]
pub struct ProcInfo {
    pub pid: u32,
    pub name: String,
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
pub struct Module {
    pub name: String,
    pub base: usize,
    pub size: usize,
}

impl Module {
    pub fn contains(&self, addr: usize) -> bool {
        addr >= self.base && addr < self.base + self.size
    }
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug)]
pub struct Region {
    pub base: usize,
    pub size: usize,
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum RegionKind {
    /// Writable data: where game values and pointers live.
    Writable,
    /// Anything readable: used for signature (AOB) search in code.
    Readable,
}

fn wstr(buf: &[u16]) -> String {
    let len = buf.iter().position(|&c| c == 0).unwrap_or(buf.len());
    String::from_utf16_lossy(&buf[..len])
}

pub fn list_processes() -> Vec<ProcInfo> {
    let mut out = Vec::new();
    unsafe {
        let snap = CreateToolhelp32Snapshot(TH32CS_SNAPPROCESS, 0);
        if snap == INVALID_HANDLE_VALUE {
            return out;
        }
        let mut e: PROCESSENTRY32W = zeroed();
        e.dwSize = size_of::<PROCESSENTRY32W>() as u32;
        if Process32FirstW(snap, &mut e) != 0 {
            loop {
                if e.th32ProcessID != 0 {
                    out.push(ProcInfo { pid: e.th32ProcessID, name: wstr(&e.szExeFile) });
                }
                if Process32NextW(snap, &mut e) == 0 {
                    break;
                }
            }
        }
        CloseHandle(snap);
    }
    out
}

pub struct Process {
    handle: HANDLE,
    pub pid: u32,
    pub name: String,
    /// 32-bit game (4-byte pointers).
    pub is32: bool,
}

// A process handle may be used from any thread.
unsafe impl Send for Process {}
unsafe impl Sync for Process {}

impl Drop for Process {
    fn drop(&mut self) {
        unsafe {
            CloseHandle(self.handle);
        }
    }
}

impl Process {
    pub fn open(pid: u32, name: &str) -> Result<Self, String> {
        unsafe {
            let h = OpenProcess(
                PROCESS_VM_READ | PROCESS_VM_WRITE | PROCESS_VM_OPERATION | PROCESS_QUERY_INFORMATION,
                0,
                pid,
            );
            if h.is_null() {
                return Err(format!(
                    "Не удалось открыть процесс (ошибка {}). Запустите FahrenHack от имени администратора.",
                    GetLastError()
                ));
            }
            let mut wow = 0;
            IsWow64Process(h, &mut wow);
            Ok(Process { handle: h, pid, name: name.to_string(), is32: wow != 0 })
        }
    }

    pub fn is_alive(&self) -> bool {
        let mut code = 0u32;
        unsafe { GetExitCodeProcess(self.handle, &mut code) != 0 && code == STILL_ACTIVE }
    }

    pub fn ptr_size(&self) -> usize {
        if self.is32 {
            4
        } else {
            8
        }
    }

    pub fn read(&self, addr: usize, buf: &mut [u8]) -> bool {
        let mut n = 0usize;
        unsafe {
            ReadProcessMemory(
                self.handle,
                addr as *const c_void,
                buf.as_mut_ptr() as *mut c_void,
                buf.len(),
                &mut n,
            ) != 0
                && n == buf.len()
        }
    }

    pub fn read_ptr(&self, addr: usize) -> Option<usize> {
        if self.is32 {
            let mut b = [0u8; 4];
            self.read(addr, &mut b).then(|| u32::from_le_bytes(b) as usize)
        } else {
            let mut b = [0u8; 8];
            self.read(addr, &mut b).then(|| u64::from_le_bytes(b) as usize)
        }
    }

    pub fn read_u32(&self, addr: usize) -> Option<u32> {
        let mut b = [0u8; 4];
        self.read(addr, &mut b).then(|| u32::from_le_bytes(b))
    }

    /// Allocates executable memory within ±1.75 GB of `near`, so a 5-byte `jmp rel32` reaches it.
    /// The memory is never freed: a game thread might still be running inside it.
    pub fn alloc_near(&self, near: usize, size: usize) -> Option<usize> {
        const GRANULARITY: usize = 0x10000;
        const RANGE: usize = 0x7000_0000;
        let lo = near.saturating_sub(RANGE).max(0x10000);
        let hi = near.saturating_add(RANGE);
        let mut addr = lo;
        unsafe {
            while addr < hi {
                let mut mbi: MEMORY_BASIC_INFORMATION = zeroed();
                if VirtualQueryEx(self.handle, addr as *const c_void, &mut mbi, size_of::<MEMORY_BASIC_INFORMATION>()) == 0 {
                    return None;
                }
                let base = mbi.BaseAddress as usize;
                let end = base.saturating_add(mbi.RegionSize);
                if mbi.State == MEM_FREE {
                    let start = (base + GRANULARITY - 1) & !(GRANULARITY - 1);
                    if start + size <= end && start + size <= hi {
                        let p = VirtualAllocEx(
                            self.handle,
                            start as *const c_void,
                            size,
                            MEM_COMMIT | MEM_RESERVE,
                            PAGE_EXECUTE_READWRITE,
                        );
                        if !p.is_null() {
                            return Some(p as usize);
                        }
                    }
                }
                if end <= addr {
                    return None;
                }
                addr = end;
            }
        }
        None
    }

    pub fn read_u64(&self, addr: usize) -> Option<u64> {
        let mut b = [0u8; 8];
        self.read(addr, &mut b).then(|| u64::from_le_bytes(b))
    }

    /// Committed stack ranges `[limit, base)` of the game's threads. Values there are
    /// short-lived copies, so scans and pointer search skip them.
    pub fn thread_stacks(&self) -> Vec<(usize, usize)> {
        let mut out = Vec::new();
        unsafe {
            let snap = CreateToolhelp32Snapshot(TH32CS_SNAPTHREAD, 0);
            if snap == INVALID_HANDLE_VALUE {
                return out;
            }
            let mut e: THREADENTRY32 = zeroed();
            e.dwSize = size_of::<THREADENTRY32>() as u32;
            if Thread32First(snap, &mut e) != 0 {
                loop {
                    if e.th32OwnerProcessID == self.pid {
                        let th = OpenThread(THREAD_QUERY_INFORMATION, 0, e.th32ThreadID);
                        if !th.is_null() {
                            let mut tbi: ThreadBasicInformation = zeroed();
                            let ok = NtQueryInformationThread(
                                th,
                                0,
                                &mut tbi as *mut _ as *mut c_void,
                                size_of::<ThreadBasicInformation>() as u32,
                                std::ptr::null_mut(),
                            ) == 0;
                            if ok {
                                let teb = tbi.teb as usize;
                                // NT_TIB: StackBase, StackLimit. A WOW64 thread keeps its 32-bit TEB 0x2000 further.
                                let range = if self.is32 {
                                    let t = teb + 0x2000;
                                    self.read_u32(t + 4).zip(self.read_u32(t + 8)).map(|(b, l)| (l as usize, b as usize))
                                } else {
                                    self.read_u64(teb + 8).zip(self.read_u64(teb + 16)).map(|(b, l)| (l as usize, b as usize))
                                };
                                if let Some((limit, base)) = range.filter(|(l, b)| b > l) {
                                    out.push((limit, base));
                                }
                            }
                            CloseHandle(th);
                        }
                    }
                    if Thread32Next(snap, &mut e) == 0 {
                        break;
                    }
                }
            }
            CloseHandle(snap);
        }
        out
    }

    pub fn write(&self, addr: usize, data: &[u8]) -> bool {
        let raw = || unsafe {
            let mut n = 0usize;
            WriteProcessMemory(
                self.handle,
                addr as *const c_void,
                data.as_ptr() as *const c_void,
                data.len(),
                &mut n,
            ) != 0
        };
        if raw() {
            return true;
        }
        // The page may be write-protected (code): lift protection for the write.
        unsafe {
            let mut old = 0u32;
            if VirtualProtectEx(self.handle, addr as *const c_void, data.len(), PAGE_EXECUTE_READWRITE, &mut old) == 0 {
                return false;
            }
            let ok = raw();
            let mut tmp = 0u32;
            VirtualProtectEx(self.handle, addr as *const c_void, data.len(), old, &mut tmp);
            ok
        }
    }

    pub fn modules(&self) -> Vec<Module> {
        let mut out = Vec::new();
        unsafe {
            let mut snap = INVALID_HANDLE_VALUE;
            for _ in 0..5 {
                snap = CreateToolhelp32Snapshot(TH32CS_SNAPMODULE | TH32CS_SNAPMODULE32, self.pid);
                if snap != INVALID_HANDLE_VALUE {
                    break;
                }
            }
            if snap == INVALID_HANDLE_VALUE {
                return out;
            }
            let mut e: MODULEENTRY32W = zeroed();
            e.dwSize = size_of::<MODULEENTRY32W>() as u32;
            if Module32FirstW(snap, &mut e) != 0 {
                loop {
                    out.push(Module {
                        name: wstr(&e.szModule),
                        base: e.modBaseAddr as usize,
                        size: e.modBaseSize as usize,
                    });
                    if Module32NextW(snap, &mut e) == 0 {
                        break;
                    }
                }
            }
            CloseHandle(snap);
        }
        out
    }

    /// Build fingerprint of a loaded module: PE TimeDateStamp + image size.
    /// Changes with every game patch, so calibrations are kept per build.
    pub fn build_key(&self, m: &Module) -> String {
        let stamp = self
            .read_u32(m.base + 0x3C)
            .and_then(|lfanew| self.read_u32(m.base + lfanew as usize + 8))
            .unwrap_or(0);
        format!("{stamp:08x}-{:x}", m.size)
    }

    /// Committed memory regions of the requested kind, optionally limited to [start, end).
    pub fn regions_in(&self, kind: RegionKind, start: usize, end: usize) -> Vec<Region> {
        let writable = PAGE_READWRITE | PAGE_WRITECOPY | PAGE_EXECUTE_READWRITE | PAGE_EXECUTE_WRITECOPY;
        let readable = writable | PAGE_READONLY | PAGE_EXECUTE_READ;
        let want = if kind == RegionKind::Writable { writable } else { readable };
        let mut out = Vec::new();
        let mut addr = start.max(0x10000);
        let limit: usize = if self.is32 { 0xFFFF_0000 } else { 0x7FFF_FFFF_FFFF };
        let end = end.min(limit);
        unsafe {
            while addr < end {
                let mut mbi: MEMORY_BASIC_INFORMATION = zeroed();
                if VirtualQueryEx(self.handle, addr as *const c_void, &mut mbi, size_of::<MEMORY_BASIC_INFORMATION>()) == 0 {
                    break;
                }
                let base = mbi.BaseAddress as usize;
                let size = mbi.RegionSize;
                if mbi.State == MEM_COMMIT
                    && mbi.Protect & want != 0
                    && mbi.Protect & PAGE_GUARD == 0
                    && (kind == RegionKind::Readable || mbi.Type != MEM_MAPPED)
                {
                    let b = base.max(start);
                    let e = (base + size).min(end);
                    if e > b {
                        out.push(Region { base: b, size: e - b });
                    }
                }
                let next = base.saturating_add(size);
                if next <= addr {
                    break;
                }
                addr = next;
            }
        }
        out
    }

    /// Writable game data: heap and static memory, without thread stacks.
    pub fn regions(&self) -> Vec<Region> {
        let stacks = self.thread_stacks();
        self.regions_in(RegionKind::Writable, 0, usize::MAX)
            .into_iter()
            .filter(|r| !stacks.iter().any(|&(lo, hi)| r.base < hi && r.base + r.size > lo))
            .collect()
    }
}

/// Reads big memory ranges in chunks and hands them to `f(address, bytes)`.
pub fn for_each_chunk(p: &Process, regions: &[Region], mut f: impl FnMut(usize, &[u8]) -> bool) {
    const CHUNK: usize = 16 << 20;
    let mut buf = vec![0u8; CHUNK];
    for r in regions {
        let mut off = 0;
        while off < r.size {
            let len = (r.size - off).min(CHUNK);
            let b = &mut buf[..len];
            if p.read(r.base + off, b) && !f(r.base + off, b) {
                return;
            }
            off += len;
        }
    }
}
```

`src/pointer.rs`:

```rs
//! Pointer chains `[[module+o0]+o1]+o2…` and a pointer scanner that finds chains
//! from the game's static memory to a value, so a cheat keeps working after a restart.

use std::collections::HashSet;

use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};

use crate::mem::{for_each_chunk, Module, Process};

#[derive(Clone, Debug, Serialize, Deserialize, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct Chain {
    pub module: String,
    pub offsets: Vec<String>,
}

/// A chain with the module base and offsets already resolved for this session.
#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
pub struct Parsed {
    base: usize,
    offsets: Vec<i64>,
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(default)]
pub struct Opts {
    pub max_depth: usize,
    pub max_offset: usize,
}

impl Default for Opts {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Opts { max_depth: 5, max_offset: 0x1000 }
    }
}

pub fn parse_hex(s: &str) -> Option<i64> {
    let s = s.trim();
    let (neg, s) = match s.strip_prefix('-') {
        Some(r) => (true, r.trim()),
        None => (false, s),
    };
    let s = s.trim_start_matches("0x").trim_start_matches("0X");
    let v = i64::from_str_radix(s, 16).ok()?;
    Some(if neg { -v } else { v })
}

pub fn hex(v: i64) -> String {
    if v < 0 {
        format!("-0x{:X}", -v)
    } else {
        format!("0x{v:X}")
    }
}

impl Chain {
    /// A value that lives directly in a module's static memory.
    pub fn static_at(m: &Module, addr: usize) -> Chain {
        Chain { module: m.name.clone(), offsets: vec![hex((addr - m.base) as i64)] }
    }

    pub fn parse(&self, modules: &[Module]) -> Option<Parsed> {
        let base = modules.iter().find(|m| m.name.eq_ignore_ascii_case(&self.module))?.base;
        let offsets = self.offsets.iter().map(|o| parse_hex(o)).collect::<Option<Vec<_>>>()?;
        (!offsets.is_empty()).then_some(Parsed { base, offsets })
    }
}

impl Parsed {
    pub fn resolve(&self, p: &Process) -> Option<usize> {
        let mut addr = (self.base as i64).wrapping_add(self.offsets[0]) as usize;
        for o in &self.offsets[1..] {
            let ptr = p.read_ptr(addr)?;
            if ptr < 0x10000 {
                return None;
            }
            addr = (ptr as i64).wrapping_add(*o) as usize;
        }
        Some(addr)
    }
}

/// `module = None` means the first offset is an absolute address.
pub fn resolve_chain(p: &Process, modules: &[Module], module: Option<&str>, offsets: &[String]) -> Option<usize> {
    let base = match module {
        Some(m) if !m.trim().is_empty() => modules.iter().find(|x| x.name.eq_ignore_ascii_case(m.trim()))?.base,
        _ => 0,
    };
    let offsets = offsets.iter().map(|s| parse_hex(s)).collect::<Option<Vec<_>>>()?;
    if offsets.is_empty() {
        return None;
    }
    Parsed { base, offsets }.resolve(p)
}

const MAX_PAIRS: usize = 120_000_000;
const MAX_FRONTIER: usize = 400_000;
const MAX_FOUND: usize = 30_000;
/// How many chains are kept per cheat.
pub const KEEP: usize = 64;

/// Finds pointer chains from `statics` (usually the game's exe) to `target`.
pub fn scan(
    p: &Process,
    modules: &[Module],
    statics: &[Module],
    target: usize,
    opts: Opts,
    progress: &dyn Fn(f32, &str),
) -> Result<Vec<Chain>, String> {
    let regions = p.regions();
    if regions.is_empty() {
        return Err("Не удалось прочитать память игры".into());
    }
    let starts: Vec<usize> = regions.iter().map(|r| r.base).collect();
    let ends: Vec<usize> = regions.iter().map(|r| r.base + r.size).collect();
    let (lo, hi) = (starts[0], *ends.last().unwrap());
    let inside = |v: usize| {
        if v < lo || v >= hi {
            return false;
        }
        let i = starts.partition_point(|&s| s <= v);
        i > 0 && v < ends[i - 1]
    };

    // 1. Every value in writable memory that looks like a pointer into writable memory.
    let total = regions.iter().map(|r| r.size as f64).sum::<f64>().max(1.0) as f32;
    let psz = p.ptr_size();
    let mut pairs: Vec<(u64, u64)> = Vec::new();
    let mut capped = false;
    let mut done = 0f32;
    progress(0.0, "Читаю память игры…");
    for_each_chunk(p, &regions, |addr, b| {
        let mut i = 0;
        while i + psz <= b.len() {
            let v = if psz == 8 {
                u64::from_le_bytes(b[i..i + 8].try_into().unwrap()) as usize
            } else {
                u32::from_le_bytes(b[i..i + 4].try_into().unwrap()) as usize
            };
            if inside(v) {
                pairs.push((v as u64, (addr + i) as u64));
            }
            i += psz;
        }
        if pairs.len() > MAX_PAIRS {
            capped = true;
            return false;
        }
        done += b.len() as f32;
        progress(0.55 * done / total, "Читаю память игры…");
        true
    });
    progress(0.56, "Сортирую указатели…");
    pairs.sort_unstable();

    // 2. Walk backwards from the target: who points at (target - offset)?
    struct Node {
        addr: usize,
        off: u32,
        parent: u32,
    }
    const ROOT: u32 = u32::MAX;
    let mut nodes = vec![Node { addr: target, off: 0, parent: ROOT }];
    let mut frontier: Vec<u32> = vec![0];
    let mut seen: HashSet<usize> = HashSet::from([target]);
    let mut found: Vec<(u32, usize, usize, u32)> = Vec::new(); // (node, static location, module, offset)
    'levels: for depth in 1..=opts.max_depth {
        progress(
            0.62 + 0.33 * (depth - 1) as f32 / opts.max_depth as f32,
            &format!("Ищу цепочки указателей: уровень {depth} из {}…", opts.max_depth),
        );
        let mut next: Vec<u32> = Vec::new();
        for &ni in &frontier {
            let t = nodes[ni as usize].addr;
            let from = t.saturating_sub(opts.max_offset) as u64;
            let mut k = pairs.partition_point(|&(v, _)| v < from);
            while k < pairs.len() && pairs[k].0 <= t as u64 {
                let (v, loc) = pairs[k];
                k += 1;
                let loc = loc as usize;
                let off = (t as u64 - v) as u32;
                if let Some(mi) = statics.iter().position(|m| m.contains(loc)) {
                    found.push((ni, loc, mi, off));
                    if found.len() >= MAX_FOUND {
                        break 'levels;
                    }
                } else if depth < opts.max_depth && seen.insert(loc) {
                    nodes.push(Node { addr: loc, off, parent: ni });
                    next.push((nodes.len() - 1) as u32);
                }
            }
        }
        if next.len() > MAX_FRONTIER {
            next.sort_by_key(|&i| nodes[i as usize].off);
            next.truncate(MAX_FRONTIER);
        }
        frontier = next;
        if frontier.is_empty() {
            break;
        }
    }
    drop(pairs);

    // 3. Shortest chains with the smallest offsets first; keep those that resolve right now.
    let mut chains: Vec<(usize, i64, Chain)> = found
        .iter()
        .map(|&(ni, loc, mi, off)| {
            let m = &statics[mi];
            let mut offs = vec![(loc - m.base) as i64, off as i64];
            let mut cur = ni;
            while nodes[cur as usize].parent != ROOT {
                offs.push(nodes[cur as usize].off as i64);
                cur = nodes[cur as usize].parent;
            }
            let sum: i64 = offs[1..].iter().sum();
            (offs.len(), sum, Chain { module: m.name.clone(), offsets: offs.iter().map(|&o| hex(o)).collect() })
        })
        .collect();
    chains.sort_by_key(|(len, sum, _)| (*len, *sum));
    progress(0.97, "Проверяю найденные цепочки…");
    let mut out = Vec::new();
    for (_, _, c) in chains {
        if c.parse(modules).and_then(|pc| pc.resolve(p)) == Some(target) {
            out.push(c);
            if out.len() >= KEEP {
                break;
            }
        }
    }
    if out.is_empty() && capped {
        return Err("Игра использует слишком много памяти для поиска указателей".into());
    }
    Ok(out)
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn hex_roundtrip() {
        assert_eq!(parse_hex("0x1A"), Some(26));
        assert_eq!(parse_hex("-0x10"), Some(-16));
        assert_eq!(parse_hex("ff"), Some(255));
        assert_eq!(hex(-16), "-0x10");
        assert_eq!(hex(4096), "0x1000");
        assert_eq!(parse_hex("zz"), None);
    }
}

```

`src/profile.rs`:

```rs
//! Game profiles (which cheats a game has and how to find them), per-build calibrations
//! and the user's own cheats.
//!
//! Built-in profiles live in `games/*.json` and are compiled into the binary.
//! Files in `%APPDATA%\FahrenHack\games\` override or add profiles without rebuilding.

use std::collections::HashMap;
use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};

use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};

use crate::pointer::{self, Chain};
use crate::scan::VType;

const BUILTIN: &[(&str, &str)] = &[
    ("re4r.json", include_str!("../games/re4r.json")),
    ("schedule1.json", include_str!("../games/schedule1.json")),
];

#[derive(Clone, Debug, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct GameProfile {
    pub id: String,
    pub title: String,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub subtitle: String,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub processes: Vec<String>,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub steam_appid: Option<u32>,
    #[serde(default = "default_accent")]
    pub accent: String,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub notes: String,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub pointer_scan: pointer::Opts,
    /// Order and icons of cheat categories; categories not listed follow in order of appearance.
    #[serde(default)]
    pub categories: Vec<CategoryDef>,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub cheats: Vec<CheatDef>,
    /// Verified pointer chains shipped with the profile: game build -> cheat id -> chains.
    #[serde(default)]
    pub builds: HashMap<String, HashMap<String, Vec<Chain>>>,
}

fn default_accent() -> String {
    "#2b4c6f".into()
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct CategoryDef {
    pub name: String,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub icon: String,
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Serialize, Deserialize, PartialEq, Eq)]
#[serde(rename_all = "lowercase")]
pub enum Kind {
    /// On/off switch: keeps the value (or the code patch) while on.
    Freeze,
    /// "Apply" button: writes the value once per press.
    Set,
    /// Game value edited in place with − / + (step) or typed in.
    Number,
    /// Setting from `min` to `max`; `off_value` switches the cheat off (multipliers).
    Slider,
    /// One of `options`, written on apply (e.g. a weapon to add).
    Select,
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct SelectOption {
    pub label: String,
    pub value: String,
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct CheatDef {
    pub id: String,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub category: String,
    pub name: String,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub description: String,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub icon: String,
    pub kind: Kind,
    pub vtype: VType,
    /// Default value. For `freeze`, `null` means "keep the value it had when switched on";
    /// for `slider`/`select`, the initial setting.
    #[serde(default)]
    pub value: Option<String>,
    /// Toggle / apply, or "increase" for number, slider and select.
    #[serde(default)]
    pub hotkey: Option<String>,
    /// "Decrease" for number, slider and select.
    #[serde(default)]
    pub hotkey_down: Option<String>,
    /// Shown behind an "i" next to the name.
    #[serde(default)]
    pub info: String,
    /// Shown behind a yellow "!" next to the name: side effects the player should know about.
    #[serde(default)]
    pub warn: String,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub step: Option<f64>,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub min: Option<f64>,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub max: Option<f64>,
    /// Slider value that means "cheat off" (patch removed, nothing frozen).
    #[serde(default)]
    pub off_value: Option<String>,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub options: Vec<SelectOption>,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub experimental: bool,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub custom: bool,
    pub locator: Locator,
}

impl CheatDef {
    pub fn step(&self) -> f64 {
        self.step.filter(|s| *s > 0.0).unwrap_or(1.0)
    }

    /// Clamps a number to `min`/`max`.
    pub fn clamp(&self, v: f64) -> f64 {
        let v = self.min.map_or(v, |m| v.max(m));
        self.max.map_or(v, |m| v.min(m))
    }

    pub fn is_off_value(&self, value: &str) -> bool {
        match (&self.off_value, crate::scan::parse_number(value)) {
            (Some(off), Ok(v)) => crate::scan::parse_number(off).is_ok_and(|o| (o - v).abs() < 1e-9),
            (Some(off), Err(_)) => off.trim() == value.trim(),
            (None, _) => false,
        }
    }
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Default, Serialize, Deserialize, PartialEq, Eq)]
#[serde(rename_all = "lowercase")]
pub enum CalMode {
    /// The number is visible in the game (money, ammo): exact search.
    #[default]
    Exact,
    /// Only a bar is visible (health): memory snapshot + increased/decreased.
    Unknown,
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(tag = "type", rename_all = "lowercase")]
pub enum Locator {
    /// Fixed pointer chain `[[module+o0]+o1]+…`.
    Pointer {
        #[serde(default)]
        module: Option<String>,
        offsets: Vec<String>,
    },
    /// Byte signature; optional RIP-relative [displacement offset, instruction length], then a chain.
    Aob {
        #[serde(default)]
        module: Option<String>,
        pattern: String,
        #[serde(default)]
        rip: Option<[usize; 2]>,
        #[serde(default)]
        add: i64,
        #[serde(default)]
        offsets: Vec<String>,
    },
    /// Code patch toggled on/off (original bytes are restored).
    Patch {
        #[serde(default)]
        module: Option<String>,
        pattern: String,
        #[serde(default)]
        offset: i64,
        bytes: String,
    },
    /// Code injection: `size` bytes at the signature (+offset) become a jump into a code cave
    /// allocated near the module. `code` is hex bytes with placeholders, see `crate::inject`.
    Inject {
        #[serde(default)]
        module: Option<String>,
        pattern: String,
        #[serde(default)]
        offset: i64,
        size: usize,
        code: String,
    },
    /// Found once on the user's machine by a guided search, then kept as pointer chains.
    Calibrate {
        #[serde(default)]
        mode: CalMode,
        #[serde(default)]
        prompt: String,
        #[serde(default)]
        steps: Vec<String>,
    },
}

impl GameProfile {
    /// Profile for any process without a ready-made profile.
    pub fn generic(exe: &str) -> GameProfile {
        GameProfile {
            id: exe.to_lowercase(),
            title: exe.trim_end_matches(".exe").trim_end_matches(".EXE").to_string(),
            subtitle: "Произвольный процесс".into(),
            processes: vec![exe.to_string()],
            steam_appid: None,
            accent: default_accent(),
            notes: "Для этой игры нет готового профиля. Найдите значения во вкладке «Сканер» и добавьте их кнопкой «В читы».".into(),
            pointer_scan: pointer::Opts::default(),
            categories: vec![],
            cheats: vec![],
            builds: HashMap::new(),
        }
    }
}

/// Reads a JSON file; tolerates the UTF-8 BOM that Notepad and PowerShell add.
pub fn read_json<T: serde::de::DeserializeOwned>(path: &Path) -> Option<T> {
    let s = std::fs::read_to_string(path).ok()?;
    serde_json::from_str(s.trim_start_matches('\u{feff}')).ok()
}

fn parse_profile(name: &str, s: &str) -> Option<GameProfile> {
    match serde_json::from_str::<GameProfile>(s.trim_start_matches('\u{feff}')) {
        Ok(p) => Some(p),
        Err(e) => {
            eprintln!("[!] Профиль {name} не загружен: {e}");
            None
        }
    }
}

pub fn builtin() -> Vec<GameProfile> {
    BUILTIN.iter().filter_map(|(n, s)| parse_profile(n, s)).collect()
}

pub fn load_all(data: &Path) -> Vec<GameProfile> {
    let mut list = builtin();
    let dir = data.join("games");
    let _ = std::fs::create_dir_all(&dir);
    if let Ok(rd) = std::fs::read_dir(&dir) {
        for e in rd.flatten() {
            let path = e.path();
            if path.extension().is_some_and(|x| x == "json") {
                let name = path.display().to_string();
                if let Some(p) = std::fs::read_to_string(&path).ok().and_then(|s| parse_profile(&name, &s)) {
                    list.retain(|x| x.id != p.id);
                    list.push(p);
                }
            }
        }
    }
    list
}

fn safe(s: &str) -> String {
    s.chars().map(|c| if c.is_ascii_alphanumeric() || "-_.".contains(c) { c } else { '_' }).collect()
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, Default, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct Calibration {
    #[serde(default)]
    pub cheats: HashMap<String, CalEntry>,
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct CalEntry {
    pub vtype: VType,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub chains: Vec<Chain>,
    /// In how many game sessions the chains led to the right value.
    #[serde(default)]
    pub confirmations: u32,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub last_pid: u32,
}

impl Calibration {
    fn path(data: &Path, game: &str, build: &str) -> PathBuf {
        data.join("calibration").join(safe(game)).join(format!("{}.json", safe(build)))
    }

    pub fn load(data: &Path, game: &str, build: &str) -> Calibration {
        read_json(&Self::path(data, game, build)).unwrap_or_default()
    }

    pub fn save(&self, data: &Path, game: &str, build: &str) -> Result<(), String> {
        let path = Self::path(data, game, build);
        if let Some(dir) = path.parent() {
            std::fs::create_dir_all(dir).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        }
        let s = serde_json::to_string_pretty(self).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        std::fs::write(path, s).map_err(|e| e.to_string())
    }
}

fn custom_path(data: &Path, game: &str) -> PathBuf {
    data.join("custom").join(format!("{}.json", safe(game)))
}

pub fn load_custom(data: &Path, game: &str) -> Vec<CheatDef> {
    read_json::<Vec<CheatDef>>(&custom_path(data, game))
        .unwrap_or_default()
        .into_iter()
        .map(|mut c| {
            c.custom = true;
            c
        })
        .collect()
}

pub fn save_custom(data: &Path, game: &str, cheats: &[CheatDef]) -> Result<(), String> {
    let path = custom_path(data, game);
    if let Some(dir) = path.parent() {
        std::fs::create_dir_all(dir).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    }
    let mine: Vec<&CheatDef> = cheats.iter().filter(|c| c.custom).collect();
    let s = serde_json::to_string_pretty(&mine).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    std::fs::write(path, s).map_err(|e| e.to_string())
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn builtin_profiles_parse() {
        let list = builtin();
        assert_eq!(list.len(), BUILTIN.len(), "every built-in profile must parse");
        for g in &list {
            assert!(!g.processes.is_empty(), "{}: no process names", g.id);
            let mut ids = std::collections::HashSet::new();
            for c in &g.cheats {
                assert!(ids.insert(&c.id), "{}: duplicate cheat id {}", g.id, c.id);
                for h in c.hotkey.iter().chain(c.hotkey_down.iter()) {
                    assert!(crate::hotkey::Hotkey::parse(h).is_some(), "{}: bad hotkey {h}", c.id);
                }
                for v in c.value.iter().chain(c.off_value.iter()) {
                    assert!(c.vtype.encode(v).is_ok(), "{}: bad value {v}", c.id);
                }
                for o in &c.options {
                    assert!(c.vtype.encode(&o.value).is_ok(), "{}: bad option {}", c.id, o.value);
                }
                if c.kind == Kind::Select {
                    assert!(!c.options.is_empty(), "{}: select without options", c.id);
                }
                if c.kind == Kind::Slider {
                    assert!(c.min.is_some() && c.max.is_some(), "{}: slider needs min and max", c.id);
                }
                if let Locator::Inject { code, size, pattern, .. } = &c.locator {
                    assert!(*size >= 5, "{}: inject needs at least 5 bytes for the jump", c.id);
                    assert!(crate::aob::parse(pattern).is_some(), "{}: bad pattern", c.id);
                    assert!(crate::inject::parse(code).is_some(), "{}: bad inject code", c.id);
                }
            }
        }
    }
}
```
