```js
  target: '<circle cx="12" cy="12" r="8"/><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="3"/><path d="M12 2v4M12 18v4M2 12h4M18 12h4"/>',
  eye: '<path d="M2 12s4-7 10-7 10 7 10 7-4 7-10 7S2 12 2 12z"/><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="3"/>',
};
export const icon = (name, cls = '') => `<svg class="ic ${cls}" viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true">${ICONS[name] || ICONS.star}</svg>`;

const lastHtml = new WeakMap();

/** Remembers the markup already present in a newly rendered element. */
export function rememberHtml(el, html) {
  lastHtml.set(el, html);
}

export function patchHtml(el, html) {
  if (!el || lastHtml.get(el) === html) return;
  const active = document.activeElement;
  const key = active && el.contains(active) ? active.dataset.k : null;
  el.innerHTML = html;
  rememberHtml(el, html);
  if (key) {
    const replacement = el.querySelector(`[data-k="${CSS.escape(key)}"]`);
    if (replacement) replacement.focus({ preventScroll: true });
  }
}

```

`src/web/api/mod.rs`:

```rs
//! Authenticated HTTP routes and request/response adapters.
mod scanner;

use std::net::SocketAddr;

use axum::extract::{ConnectInfo, Path, Request, State};
use axum::http::{header, StatusCode};
use axum::middleware::{self, Next};
use axum::response::{IntoResponse, Response};
use axum::routing::{delete, get, post, put};
use axum::{Json, Router};
use serde::Deserialize;
use serde_json::{json, Value};

use crate::app::{self, lock, Shared};
use crate::profile::Kind;
use crate::scan::VType;
use crate::{mem, pointer, steam};

pub(super) fn router(app: Shared) -> Router<Shared> {
    Router::new()
        .route("/state", get(state))
        .route("/job", get(job_state))
        .route("/games", get(games))
        .route("/games/{id}/launch", post(launch))
        .route(
            "/games/{gid}/cheats/{cid}",
            put(set_cheat_settings).delete(reset_cheat_settings),
        )
        .route("/processes", get(processes))
        .route("/attach", post(attach))
        .route("/detach", post(detach))
        .route("/cheats/off", post(all_off))
        .route("/cheats/{id}/toggle", post(toggle))
        .route("/cheats/{id}/value", post(set_value))
        .route("/cheats/{id}/step", post(step))
        .route("/games/{gid}/cheats/{cid}/pin", post(pin))
        .route("/cheats/{id}/calibration", delete(reset_calibration))
        .route("/calib/{id}/bind", post(bind))
        .route("/custom", post(add_custom))
        .route("/custom/{id}", delete(remove_custom))
        .merge(scanner::router())
        .route("/settings", put(put_settings))
        .route("/token/reset", post(reset_token))
        .route("/open-data", post(open_data))
        .route("/support", post(open_support))
        .route("/qr", get(qr))
        .route("/art/{game}/{kind}", get(art))
        .layer(middleware::from_fn_with_state(app, auth))
}

type ApiResult = Result<Json<Value>, (StatusCode, String)>;

fn bad(e: impl ToString) -> (StatusCode, String) {
    (StatusCode::BAD_REQUEST, e.to_string())
}

fn ok() -> ApiResult {
    Ok(Json(json!({"ok": true})))
}

fn job(id: Result<u64, String>) -> ApiResult {
    Ok(Json(json!({"job": id.map_err(bad)?})))
}

async fn auth(State(app): State<Shared>, req: Request, next: Next) -> Response {
    let (token, remote) = {
        let a = lock(&app);
        (a.settings.token.clone(), a.settings.remote)
    };
    let local = req
        .extensions()
        .get::<ConnectInfo<SocketAddr>>()
        .is_some_and(|c| c.0.ip().is_loopback());
    if !local && !remote {
        return (
            StatusCode::FORBIDDEN,
            "Пульт выключен в настройках FahrenHack",
        )
            .into_response();
    }
    let by_header =
        req.headers().get("x-token").and_then(|v| v.to_str().ok()) == Some(token.as_str());
    let by_query = req.uri().query().is_some_and(|q| {
        q.split('&')
            .any(|kv| kv.strip_prefix("t=") == Some(token.as_str()))
    });
    if by_header || by_query {
        next.run(req).await
    } else {
        (
            StatusCode::UNAUTHORIZED,
            "Неверный токен: откройте ссылку или QR-код из FahrenHack",
        )
            .into_response()
    }
}

async fn state(State(app): State<Shared>) -> ApiResult {
    Ok(Json(lock(&app).state_json()))
}

async fn job_state(State(app): State<Shared>) -> ApiResult {
    Ok(Json(json!(lock(&app).job.clone())))
}

async fn games(State(app): State<Shared>) -> ApiResult {
    Ok(Json(lock(&app).games_json()))
}

async fn launch(State(app): State<Shared>, Path(id): Path<String>) -> ApiResult {
    let appid = lock(&app)
        .steam_appid(&id)
        .ok_or_else(|| bad("Игру нельзя запустить через Steam"))?;
    steam::launch(appid).map_err(bad)?;
    ok()
}

#[derive(Deserialize)]
struct CheatSettings {
    #[serde(default)]
    value: String,
    #[serde(default)]
    hotkey: String,
    /// Absent = keep; "" = no second hotkey.
    #[serde(default)]
    hotkey_down: Option<String>,
}

async fn set_cheat_settings(
    State(app): State<Shared>,
    Path((gid, cid)): Path<(String, String)>,
    Json(b): Json<CheatSettings>,
) -> ApiResult {
    lock(&app)
        .set_override(&gid, &cid, b.value, b.hotkey, b.hotkey_down)
        .map_err(bad)?;
    ok()
}

#[derive(Deserialize)]
struct ValueReq {
    value: String,
}

async fn set_value(
    State(app): State<Shared>,
    Path(id): Path<String>,
    Json(r): Json<ValueReq>,
) -> ApiResult {
    let on = lock(&app).set_value(&id, &r.value).map_err(bad)?;
    Ok(Json(json!({"active": on})))
}

#[derive(Deserialize)]
struct StepReq {
    dir: i32,
}

async fn step(State(app): State<Shared>, Path(id): Path<String>, Json(r): Json<StepReq>) -> ApiResult {
    let on = lock(&app).step(&id, r.dir).map_err(bad)?;
    Ok(Json(json!({"active": on})))
}

async fn pin(State(app): State<Shared>, Path((gid, cid)): Path<(String, String)>) -> ApiResult {
    let pinned = lock(&app).toggle_pin(&gid, &cid);
    Ok(Json(json!({"pinned": pinned})))
}

async fn reset_cheat_settings(
    State(app): State<Shared>,
    Path((gid, cid)): Path<(String, String)>,
) -> ApiResult {
    lock(&app).clear_override(&gid, &cid);
    ok()
}

async fn processes() -> ApiResult {
    let mut list = mem::list_processes();
    list.sort_by_key(|p| p.name.to_lowercase());
    Ok(Json(json!(list)))
}

#[derive(Deserialize)]
struct AttachReq {
    pid: u32,
}

async fn attach(State(app): State<Shared>, Json(r): Json<AttachReq>) -> ApiResult {
    let name = mem::list_processes()
        .into_iter()
        .find(|p| p.pid == r.pid)
        .map(|p| p.name)
        .ok_or_else(|| bad("Процесс не найден"))?;
    lock(&app).attach(r.pid, &name).map_err(bad)?;
    ok()
}

async fn detach(State(app): State<Shared>) -> ApiResult {
    lock(&app).detach_manually();
    ok()
}

async fn all_off(State(app): State<Shared>) -> ApiResult {
    lock(&app).all_off();
    ok()
}

async fn toggle(State(app): State<Shared>, Path(id): Path<String>) -> ApiResult {
    let on = lock(&app).toggle(&id).map_err(bad)?;
    Ok(Json(json!({"active": on})))
}

async fn reset_calibration(State(app): State<Shared>, Path(id): Path<String>) -> ApiResult {
    lock(&app).reset_calibration(&id).map_err(bad)?;
    ok()
}

#[derive(Deserialize)]
struct BindReq {
    addr: String,
    vtype: VType,
}

async fn bind(
    State(app): State<Shared>,
    Path(id): Path<String>,
    Json(r): Json<BindReq>,
) -> ApiResult {
    let addr = pointer::parse_hex(&r.addr)
        .filter(|a| *a > 0)
        .ok_or_else(|| bad("Неверный адрес"))? as usize;
    job(app::start_bind(&app, id, addr, r.vtype))
}

#[derive(Deserialize)]
struct CustomReq {
    name: String,
    vtype: VType,
    kind: Kind,
    #[serde(default)]
    value: Option<String>,
}

async fn add_custom(State(app): State<Shared>, Json(r): Json<CustomReq>) -> ApiResult {
    let id = lock(&app)
        .add_custom(r.name, r.vtype, r.kind, r.value)
        .map_err(bad)?;
    Ok(Json(json!({"id": id})))
}

async fn remove_custom(State(app): State<Shared>, Path(id): Path<String>) -> ApiResult {
    lock(&app).remove_custom(&id).map_err(bad)?;
    ok()
}

#[derive(Deserialize)]
struct SettingsReq {
    auto_attach: Option<bool>,
    hotkeys: Option<bool>,
    remote: Option<bool>,
    sounds: Option<bool>,
    fullscreen: Option<bool>,
}

async fn put_settings(State(app): State<Shared>, Json(r): Json<SettingsReq>) -> ApiResult {
    let mut a = lock(&app);
    if let Some(v) = r.auto_attach {
        a.settings.auto_attach = v;
    }
    if let Some(v) = r.hotkeys {
        a.settings.hotkeys = v;
    }
    if let Some(v) = r.remote {
        a.settings.remote = v;
    }
    if let Some(v) = r.sounds {
        a.settings.sounds = v;
    }
    if let Some(v) = r.fullscreen {
        a.settings.fullscreen = v;
    }
    a.save_settings();
    ok()
}

async fn reset_token(State(app): State<Shared>) -> ApiResult {
    let mut a = lock(&app);
    a.settings.token = app::gen_token();
    a.save_settings();
    Ok(Json(
        json!({"token": a.settings.token, "lan_url": a.lan_url()}),
    ))
}

/// Project's Ko-fi page (also in `.github/FUNDING.yml`).
const SUPPORT_URL: &str = "https://ko-fi.com/fahrentech";

/// The desktop window cannot show external sites, so the page opens in the default browser.
/// The URL is fixed here: the API never opens addresses sent by the client.
async fn open_support() -> ApiResult {
    steam::open(SUPPORT_URL).map_err(bad)?;
    ok()
}

async fn open_data(State(app): State<Shared>) -> ApiResult {
    let dir = lock(&app).data_dir.display().to_string();
    steam::open(&dir).map_err(bad)?;
    ok()
}

async fn qr(State(app): State<Shared>) -> Response {
    let url = lock(&app).lan_url();
    match qrcode::QrCode::new(url.as_bytes()) {
        Ok(code) => {
            let svg = code
                .render::<qrcode::render::svg::Color>()
                .min_dimensions(240, 240)
                .dark_color(qrcode::render::svg::Color("#0b1626"))
                .light_color(qrcode::render::svg::Color("#ffffff"))
                .build();
            ([(header::CONTENT_TYPE, "image/svg+xml")], svg).into_response()
        }
        Err(e) => bad(e).into_response(),
    }
}

async fn art(State(app): State<Shared>, Path((game, kind)): Path<(String, String)>) -> Response {
    let appid = lock(&app).steam_appid(&game);
    let Some(path) = appid.and_then(|a| steam::art(a, &kind)) else {
        return StatusCode::NOT_FOUND.into_response();
    };
    match std::fs::read(&path) {
        Ok(bytes) => {
            let ct = if path
                .extension()
                .is_some_and(|e| e.eq_ignore_ascii_case("png"))
            {
                "image/png"
            } else {
                "image/jpeg"
            };
            (
                [
                    (header::CONTENT_TYPE, ct),
                    (header::CACHE_CONTROL, "max-age=3600"),
                ],
                bytes,
            )
                .into_response()
        }
        Err(_) => StatusCode::NOT_FOUND.into_response(),
    }
}
```

```rs
//! HTTP adapters for the scanner and its result list.
use axum::extract::State;
use axum::routing::{get, post};
use axum::{Json, Router};
use serde::Deserialize;
use serde_json::json;

use crate::app::{self, lock, ScanReq, Shared};
use crate::scan::{self, NextMode, VType};

use super::{bad, job, ok, ApiResult};

pub(super) fn router() -> Router<Shared> {
    Router::new()
        .route("/scan/first", post(scan_first))
        .route("/scan/unknown", post(scan_unknown))
        .route("/scan/next", post(scan_next))
        .route("/scan/reset", post(scan_reset))
        .route("/scan/results", get(scan_results))
        .route("/write", post(write_mem))
}

#[derive(Deserialize)]
struct FirstReq {
    vtype: VType,
    value: String,
}

async fn scan_first(State(app): State<Shared>, Json(r): Json<FirstReq>) -> ApiResult {
    let value = scan::parse_number(&r.value).map_err(bad)?;
    job(app::start_scan(
        &app,
        ScanReq::First {
            vtype: r.vtype,
            value,
        },
    ))
}

#[derive(Deserialize)]
struct UnknownReq {
    vtype: VType,
}

async fn scan_unknown(State(app): State<Shared>, Json(r): Json<UnknownReq>) -> ApiResult {
    job(app::start_scan(&app, ScanReq::Unknown { vtype: r.vtype }))
}

#[derive(Deserialize)]
struct NextReq {
    mode: NextMode,
    #[serde(default)]
    value: Option<String>,
}

async fn scan_next(State(app): State<Shared>, Json(r): Json<NextReq>) -> ApiResult {
    let value = match r.value.as_deref().map(str::trim) {
        Some(v) if !v.is_empty() => Some(scan::parse_number(v).map_err(bad)?),
        _ => None,
    };
    job(app::start_scan(
        &app,
        ScanReq::Next {
            mode: r.mode,
            value,
        },
    ))
}

async fn scan_reset(State(app): State<Shared>) -> ApiResult {
    lock(&app).reset_scan().map_err(bad)?;
    ok()
}

async fn scan_results(State(app): State<Shared>) -> ApiResult {
    Ok(Json(json!(lock(&app).scan_results())))
}

#[derive(Deserialize)]
struct WriteReq {
    addr: String,
    vtype: VType,
    value: String,
}

async fn write_mem(State(app): State<Shared>, Json(r): Json<WriteReq>) -> ApiResult {
    lock(&app)
        .write_scan_value(&r.addr, r.vtype, &r.value)
        .map_err(bad)?;
    ok()
}
```

`src/web/assets.rs`:

```rs
//! UI assets packaged with the executable.
use axum::http::header;
use axum::response::IntoResponse;
use axum::routing::get;
use axum::Router;

use crate::app::Shared;

const HTML: &str = include_str!("../ui/index.html");
const JAVASCRIPT: &str = "text/javascript; charset=utf-8";
const ASSETS: &[(&str, &str, &str)] = &[
    ("/", "text/html; charset=utf-8", HTML),
    (
        "/app.css",
        "text/css; charset=utf-8",
        include_str!("../ui/app.css"),
    ),
    ("/app.js", JAVASCRIPT, include_str!("../ui/app.js")),
    ("/ui/api.js", JAVASCRIPT, include_str!("../ui/api.js")),
    (
        "/ui/view-helpers.js",
        JAVASCRIPT,
        include_str!("../ui/view-helpers.js"),
    ),
    (
        "/ui/cheat-list.js",
        JAVASCRIPT,
        include_str!("../ui/cheat-list.js"),
    ),
    (
        "/logo.svg",
        "image/svg+xml",
        include_str!("../../assets/logo.svg"),
    ),
];

pub(super) fn router() -> Router<Shared> {
    let mut router = Router::new();
    for &(path, content_type, body) in ASSETS {
        router = router.route(
            path,
            get(move || async move {
                (
                    [
                        (header::CONTENT_TYPE, content_type),
                        (header::CACHE_CONTROL, "no-cache"),
                    ],
                    body,
                )
                    .into_response()
            }),
        );
    }
    router
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::{ASSETS, HTML, JAVASCRIPT};

    // The desktop host defines window.ipc as a non-configurable global.
    #[test]
    fn ui_scripts_cannot_clash_with_webview_globals() {
        assert!(
            HTML.contains(r#"<script type="module" src="/app.js">"#),
            "app.js must load as a module"
        );
        for &(path, _, source) in ASSETS.iter().filter(|asset| asset.1 == JAVASCRIPT) {
            for declaration in ["const ipc", "let ipc", "var ipc", "function ipc"] {
                assert!(
                    !source.contains(declaration),
                    "{path} must not declare `{declaration}`"
                );
            }
        }
    }
}
```

`src/web/mod.rs`:

```rs
//! HTTP listeners for the desktop window and the optional phone remote.
mod api;
mod assets;

use std::net::SocketAddr;
use std::time::Duration;

use axum::Router;

use crate::app::{self, lock, Shared};

pub async fn serve(app: Shared, ready: std::sync::mpsc::Sender<Result<u16, String>>) {
    let first = lock(&app).settings.port;
    let mut bound = None;
    for port in first..first.saturating_add(10) {
        if let Ok(l) = tokio::net::TcpListener::bind(("127.0.0.1", port)).await {
            bound = Some((l, port));
            break;
        }
    }
    let Some((listener, port)) = bound else {
        let _ = ready.send(Err(format!(
            "Порты {first}–{} заняты",
            first.saturating_add(9)
        )));
        return;
    };

    let router = assets::router()
        .nest("/api", api::router(app.clone()))
        .with_state(app.clone());
    let _ = ready.send(Ok(port));
    tokio::spawn(lan_listener(app, router.clone(), port));
    let _ = axum::serve(
        listener,
        router.into_make_service_with_connect_info::<SocketAddr>(),
    )
    .await;
}

/// Listens on the LAN address only while the phone remote is switched on.
async fn lan_listener(app: Shared, router: Router, port: u16) {
    let mut running: Option<(String, tokio::task::JoinHandle<()>)> = None;
    let mut last_error = String::new();
    loop {
        let want = lock(&app).settings.remote;
        if want {
            let ip = app::detect_lan_ip();
            lock(&app).lan_ip = ip.clone();
            let same = running.as_ref().is_some_and(|(r, _)| *r == ip);
            if !same && ip != "127.0.0.1" {
                if let Some((_, h)) = running.take() {
                    h.abort();
                }
                match tokio::net::TcpListener::bind((ip.as_str(), port)).await {
                    Ok(l) => {
                        println!("[+] Пульт для телефона: http://{ip}:{port}");
                        let r = router.clone();
                        let h = tokio::spawn(async move {
                            let _ = axum::serve(
                                l,
                                r.into_make_service_with_connect_info::<SocketAddr>(),
                            )
                            .await;
                        });
                        running = Some((ip, h));
                        last_error.clear();
                    }
                    Err(e) => {
                        let msg = format!("[!] Пульт: не удалось открыть {ip}:{port}: {e}");
                        if msg != last_error {
                            eprintln!("{msg}");
                            last_error = msg;
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        } else if let Some((_, h)) = running.take() {
            h.abort();
            println!("[-] Пульт выключен");
        }
        tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_millis(800)).await;
    }
}

```

`src/window.rs`:

```rs
//! Native desktop window: a frameless WebView2 window that shows the console UI.

use std::path::PathBuf;

use tao::dpi::LogicalSize;
use tao::event::{Event, WindowEvent};
use tao::event_loop::{ControlFlow, EventLoopBuilder};
use tao::window::{Fullscreen, Icon, WindowBuilder};
use wry::{WebContext, WebViewBuilder};

#[derive(Debug)]
enum UiEvent {
    Drag,
    Minimize,
    Maximize,
    Fullscreen,
    Close,
}

/// Opens the window and runs the event loop. Only returns if the window could not be created.
pub fn run(url: String, data_dir: PathBuf, fullscreen: bool, on_exit: Box<dyn FnOnce()>) -> Result<(), String> {
    let event_loop = EventLoopBuilder::<UiEvent>::with_user_event().build();
    let proxy = event_loop.create_proxy();
    let icon = Icon::from_rgba(crate::logo::logo_rgba(64), 64, 64).ok();
    let window = WindowBuilder::new()
        .with_title("FahrenHack")
        .with_decorations(false)
        .with_inner_size(LogicalSize::new(1280.0, 800.0))
        .with_min_inner_size(LogicalSize::new(960.0, 600.0))
        .with_window_icon(icon)
        .with_visible(false)
        .build(&event_loop)
        .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    if fullscreen {
        window.set_fullscreen(Some(Fullscreen::Borderless(None)));
    }

    let mut context = WebContext::new(Some(data_dir.join("webview")));
    let webview = WebViewBuilder::new_with_web_context(&mut context)
        .with_url(url)
        .with_background_color((6, 12, 21, 255))
        .with_devtools(cfg!(debug_assertions))
        .with_ipc_handler(move |req| {
            let ev = match req.body().as_str() {
                "drag" => UiEvent::Drag,
                "min" => UiEvent::Minimize,
                "max" => UiEvent::Maximize,
                "fullscreen" => UiEvent::Fullscreen,
                "close" => UiEvent::Close,
                _ => return,
            };
            let _ = proxy.send_event(ev);
        })
        .build(&window)
        .map_err(|e| format!("WebView2: {e}"))?;
    window.set_visible(true);

    let mut on_exit = Some(on_exit);
    event_loop.run(move |event, _, control_flow| {
        *control_flow = ControlFlow::Wait;
        let _keep_alive = (&webview, &context);
        match event {
            Event::WindowEvent { event: WindowEvent::CloseRequested, .. } | Event::UserEvent(UiEvent::Close) => {
                if let Some(f) = on_exit.take() {
                    f();
                }
                *control_flow = ControlFlow::Exit;
            }
            Event::UserEvent(UiEvent::Drag) => {
                let _ = window.drag_window();
            }
            Event::UserEvent(UiEvent::Minimize) => window.set_minimized(true),
            Event::UserEvent(UiEvent::Maximize) => window.set_maximized(!window.is_maximized()),
            Event::UserEvent(UiEvent::Fullscreen) => {
                if window.fullscreen().is_some() {
                    window.set_fullscreen(None);
                } else {
                    window.set_fullscreen(Some(Fullscreen::Borderless(None)));
                }
            }
            _ => {}
        }
    });
}

```

`tools/cdp.ps1`:

```ps1
# Minimal Chrome DevTools Protocol client for the WebView2 inside the FahrenHack window.
# Start the app with $env:WEBVIEW2_ADDITIONAL_BROWSER_ARGUMENTS = '--remote-debugging-port=9223', then:
#   . .\tools\cdp.ps1 ; $ws = Get-CdpPage 9223 ; Invoke-CdpEval $ws 'document.title'

function Get-CdpPage([int]$port) {
  for ($i = 0; $i -lt 40; $i++) {
    try {
      $t = @(Invoke-RestMethod "http://127.0.0.1:$port/json" -TimeoutSec 2 | ForEach-Object { $_ }) |
        Where-Object { $_.type -eq 'page' -and $_.url -like 'http://127.0.0.1:*' } | Select-Object -First 1
      if ($t) { return $t.webSocketDebuggerUrl }
    } catch { }
    Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 250
  }
  throw "no CDP page on port $port"
}

function Invoke-Cdp([string]$ws, [string]$method, $params = @{}) {
  $client = [System.Net.WebSockets.ClientWebSocket]::new()
  $ct = [Threading.CancellationToken]::None
  $client.ConnectAsync([Uri]$ws, $ct).Wait()
  $msg = @{ id = 1; method = $method; params = $params } | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 20 -Compress
  $bytes = [Text.Encoding]::UTF8.GetBytes($msg)
  $client.SendAsync([ArraySegment[byte]]::new($bytes), [System.Net.WebSockets.WebSocketMessageType]::Text, $true, $ct).Wait()
  $buf = New-Object byte[] 1048576
  $ms = New-Object System.IO.MemoryStream
  $obj = $null
  while ($true) {
    $res = $client.ReceiveAsync([ArraySegment[byte]]::new($buf), $ct).Result
    $ms.Write($buf, 0, $res.Count)
    if ($res.EndOfMessage) {
      $text = [Text.Encoding]::UTF8.GetString($ms.ToArray())
      $ms.SetLength(0)
      $obj = $text | ConvertFrom-Json
      if ($obj.id -eq 1) { break }
    }
  }
  $client.Dispose()
  return $obj
}

function Invoke-CdpEval([string]$ws, [string]$expr) {
  $r = Invoke-Cdp $ws 'Runtime.evaluate' @{ expression = $expr; returnByValue = $true; awaitPromise = $true }
  if ($r.result.exceptionDetails) { return "EXCEPTION: " + $r.result.exceptionDetails.exception.description }
  return $r.result.result.value
}

function Save-CdpScreenshot([string]$ws, [string]$path) {
  $r = Invoke-Cdp $ws 'Page.captureScreenshot' @{ format = 'png' }
  [IO.File]::WriteAllBytes($path, [Convert]::FromBase64String($r.result.data))
}
```

`tools/dummy-game/src/main.rs`:

```rs
//! Test "game" for FahrenHack end-to-end checks.
//! HP lives behind a real pointer chain: static WORLD -> World (+0x40) -> Player (+0x18) -> hp.
//! HP drops by 7 every second, ammo by 1 every 3 seconds; both are printed each tick.

use std::hint::black_box;
use std::sync::atomic::{AtomicI32, AtomicPtr, Ordering};

#[repr(C)]
struct Player {
    pad: [u64; 3],
    hp: AtomicI32,
    ammo: AtomicI32,
}

#[repr(C)]
struct World {
    pad: [u8; 0x40],
    player: Box<Player>,
}

static WORLD: AtomicPtr<World> = AtomicPtr::new(std::ptr::null_mut());

/// Separate function so tests can patch it with a code injection
/// (compiles to `lock sub [rcx+18h], edx ; ret`).
#[inline(never)]
fn take_damage(p: &Player, dmg: i32) {
    p.hp.fetch_sub(dmg, Ordering::SeqCst);
}

fn main() {
    let w = Box::new(World {
        pad: [0; 0x40],
        player: Box::new(Player { pad: [0; 3], hp: AtomicI32::new(1000), ammo: AtomicI32::new(30) }),
    });
    WORLD.store(Box::into_raw(w), Ordering::SeqCst);
    let mut tick = 0u64;
    loop {
        let w = unsafe { &*black_box(WORLD.load(Ordering::SeqCst)) };
        println!("hp={} ammo={}", w.player.hp.load(Ordering::SeqCst), w.player.ammo.load(Ordering::SeqCst));
        tick += 1;
        take_damage(&w.player, black_box(7));
        if tick % 3 == 0 {
            let a = w.player.ammo.load(Ordering::SeqCst);
            w.player.ammo.store(if a <= 1 { 30 } else { a - 1 }, Ordering::SeqCst);
        }
        std::thread::sleep(std::time::Duration::from_millis(1000));
    }
}

```

`tools/e2e.ps1`:

```ps1
# End-to-end check of the cheat engine against tools/dummy-game (no window, HTTP API only):
# auto-attach, hidden-value calibration (snapshot + decreased), pointer scan, freeze,
# exact-value calibration, set, restart persistence, re-confirmation, settings override.
#   powershell -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File tools\e2e.ps1
# Builds the debug app and the dummy game first. Uses a temporary data dir.
# Keep this file ASCII-only: Windows PowerShell 5.1 reads BOM-less scripts as ANSI.
$ErrorActionPreference = 'Stop'
$root = (Resolve-Path "$PSScriptRoot\..").Path
Get-Process fahrenhack, dummygame2 -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue | Stop-Process -Force
Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 800

Push-Location $root
cargo build -q
if ($LASTEXITCODE) { throw 'cargo build failed' }
cargo build -q --release --manifest-path tools\dummy-game\Cargo.toml --target-dir target\dummy
if ($LASTEXITCODE) { throw 'dummy game build failed' }
Pop-Location

$exe = "$root\target\debug\fahrenhack.exe"
$dummy = "$root\target\dummy\release\dummygame2.exe"
$data = Join-Path $env:TEMP 'fahrenhack-e2e'
$log = Join-Path $env:TEMP 'fahrenhack-e2e.log'
$out = Join-Path $env:TEMP 'fahrenhack-e2e-game.txt'
Remove-Item -Recurse -Force $data -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force "$data\games" | Out-Null
$profile = @'
{
  "id": "dummy", "title": "Dummy Game", "subtitle": "test", "processes": ["dummygame2.exe"], "accent": "#2f8f6a",
  "pointer_scan": { "max_depth": 4, "max_offset": 4096 },
  "categories": [ { "name": "Player", "icon": "user" }, { "name": "Weapons", "icon": "gun" } ],
  "cheats": [
    { "id": "hp", "category": "Player", "icon": "heart", "name": "HP", "kind": "freeze", "vtype": "i32", "value": null, "hotkey": "F9",
      "locator": { "type": "calibrate", "mode": "unknown", "steps": ["snapshot", "decreased"] } },
    { "id": "ammo", "category": "Weapons", "icon": "ammo", "name": "Ammo", "kind": "number", "vtype": "i32",
      "min": 0, "max": 999, "step": 1, "hotkey": "F7", "hotkey_down": "SHIFT+F7",
      "locator": { "type": "calibrate", "mode": "exact" } },
    { "id": "god", "category": "Player", "icon": "shield", "name": "No damage", "kind": "freeze", "vtype": "i32", "info": "test",
      "locator": { "type": "inject", "pattern": "F0 29 51 18 C3 CC", "size": 5, "code": "C3" } },
    { "id": "dmg", "category": "Player", "icon": "skull", "name": "Damage per hit", "kind": "slider", "vtype": "i32",
      "min": 0, "max": 50, "step": 1, "off_value": "7", "warn": "test",
      "locator": { "type": "inject", "pattern": "F0 29 51 18 C3 CC", "size": 5, "code": "8B 15 {param} F0 29 51 18 C3" } },
    { "id": "dmgsel", "category": "Player", "icon": "skull", "name": "Damage preset", "kind": "select", "vtype": "i32",
      "options": [ { "label": "light", "value": "1" }, { "label": "heavy", "value": "100" } ],
      "locator": { "type": "inject", "pattern": "F0 29 51 18 C3 CC", "size": 5, "code": "8B 15 {param} F0 29 51 18 C3" } }
  ]
}
'@
[IO.File]::WriteAllText("$data\games\dummy.json", $profile)

$env:FAHRENHACK_DATA = $data
$t = Start-Process $exe -ArgumentList '--headless' -PassThru -WindowStyle Hidden -RedirectStandardOutput $log
$g = Start-Process $dummy -PassThru -WindowStyle Hidden -RedirectStandardOutput $out
$fail = 0
function Check($ok, $what) { if ($ok) { "PASS  $what" } else { "FAIL  $what"; $script:fail++ } }
try {
  Start-Sleep 4
  $port = [regex]::Match((Get-Content $log -Raw -Encoding utf8), '127\.0\.0\.1:(\d+)').Groups[1].Value
  $token = (Get-Content "$data\settings.json" -Raw | ConvertFrom-Json).token
  $h = @{ 'x-token' = $token }
  $b = "http://127.0.0.1:$port/api"
  function J($m, $p, $body) {
    if ($null -ne $body) { Invoke-RestMethod "$b$p" -Method $m -Headers $h -ContentType 'application/json; charset=utf-8' -Body ([Text.Encoding]::UTF8.GetBytes(($body | ConvertTo-Json -Compress))) }
    else { Invoke-RestMethod "$b$p" -Method $m -Headers $h }
  }
  function WaitJob($r) {
    while ($true) { Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 200; $s = J GET /job; if ($s.id -eq $r.job -and $s.done) { if ($s.error) { throw "job: $($s.error)" }; return $s.result } }
  }
  function Last { Get-Content $out -Tail 1 }
  function Ammo { [int]([regex]::Match((Last), 'ammo=(\d+)').Groups[1].Value) }
  function Hp { [int]([regex]::Match((Last), 'hp=(-?\d+)').Groups[1].Value) }

  $denied = $false; try { Invoke-RestMethod "$b/state" | Out-Null } catch { $denied = $true }
  Check $denied 'API rejects requests without the token'
  $s = J GET /state
  Check ($s.attached.exe -eq 'dummygame2.exe') "auto-attached to the game (build $($s.attached.build))"

  # HP: hidden value -> snapshot + decreased
  WaitJob (J POST /scan/unknown @{ vtype = 'i32' }) | Out-Null
  foreach ($i in 1..3) { Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 1300; $r = WaitJob (J POST /scan/next @{ mode = 'decreased' }) }
  $res = @(J GET /scan/results | ForEach-Object { $_ })
  Check ($res.Count -ge 1 -and $res.Count -le 3) "hidden-value search narrowed to $($res.Count) candidate(s)"
  $r = WaitJob (J POST /calib/hp/bind @{ addr = $res[0].addr; vtype = 'i32' })
  Check ($r.chains -gt 0) "pointer scan found $($r.chains) chain(s)"
  $cal = Get-Content (Get-ChildItem "$data\calibration\dummy\*.json").FullName -Raw | ConvertFrom-Json
  $first = $cal.cheats.hp.chains[0]
  Check (($first.offsets | Select-Object -Skip 1) -join ',' -eq '0x40,0x18') "best chain is $($first.module) $($first.offsets -join ' -> ')"
  J POST /cheats/hp/toggle | Out-Null
  Start-Sleep 3
  $hp1 = Hp; Start-Sleep 2; $hp2 = Hp
  Check ($hp1 -eq $hp2) "freeze holds HP ($hp1 = $hp2)"

  # Ammo: visible value -> exact
  # The game changes ammo on its own timer, so a scan can race with it: retry a few times.
  $res = @()
  for ($try = 1; $try -le 3 -and $res.Count -eq 0; $try++) {
    $a1 = Ammo
    $deadline = (Get-Date).AddSeconds(8)
    while ((Ammo) -eq $a1 -and (Get-Date) -lt $deadline) { Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 200 }  # start right after a change
    $a1 = Ammo
    WaitJob (J POST /scan/first @{ vtype = 'i32'; value = "$a1" }) | Out-Null
    $deadline = (Get-Date).AddSeconds(8)
    while ((Ammo) -eq $a1 -and (Get-Date) -lt $deadline) { Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 200 }
    WaitJob (J POST /scan/next @{ mode = 'exact'; value = "$(Ammo)" }) | Out-Null
    $res = @(J GET /scan/results | ForEach-Object { $_ })
  }
  Check ($res.Count -ge 1) "exact search found ammo ($($res.Count) candidate(s))"
  WaitJob (J POST /calib/ammo/bind @{ addr = $res[0].addr; vtype = 'i32' }) | Out-Null

  # Number: type a value, step up and down, clamp to max
  J POST /cheats/ammo/value @{ value = '50' } | Out-Null
  Start-Sleep 1.2
  Check ((Ammo) -eq 50) "number writes ammo = 50 (game shows $(Ammo))"
  J POST /cheats/ammo/step @{ dir = 1 } | Out-Null; J POST /cheats/ammo/step @{ dir = 1 } | Out-Null; J POST /cheats/ammo/step @{ dir = -1 } | Out-Null
  Start-Sleep 1.2
  Check ((Ammo) -eq 51 -or (Ammo) -eq 50) "number steps +1 +1 -1 (game shows $(Ammo))"
  J POST /cheats/ammo/value @{ value = '5000' } | Out-Null
  Start-Sleep 1.2
  Check ((Ammo) -eq 999) "number is clamped to max 999 (game shows $(Ammo))"

  # Code injection on take_damage(); HP freeze must be off so damage is visible
  J POST /cheats/hp/toggle | Out-Null
  function Dmg { Start-Sleep 2.3; $l = @(Get-Content $out -Tail 2); [int]([regex]::Match($l[0], 'hp=(-?\d+)').Groups[1].Value) - [int]([regex]::Match($l[1], 'hp=(-?\d+)').Groups[1].Value) }
  Check ((Dmg) -eq 7) 'original damage is 7 per tick'
  $r = J POST /cheats/god/toggle
  Check ($r.active -and (Dmg) -eq 0) 'inject: no-damage patch stops damage'
  $conflict = ''; try { J POST /cheats/dmg/value @{ value = '20' } | Out-Null } catch { $conflict = $_.ErrorDetails.Message }
  Check ($conflict -match 'No damage') "two patches of the same code are refused ($conflict)"
  $r = J POST /cheats/god/toggle
  Check (-not $r.active -and (Dmg) -eq 7) 'inject off restores the original code'
  $r = J POST /cheats/dmg/value @{ value = '20' }
  Check ($r.active -and (Dmg) -eq 20) 'slider: injected code reads the parameter (20 per tick)'
  J POST /cheats/dmg/step @{ dir = 1 } | Out-Null
  Check ((Dmg) -eq 21) 'slider step changes the live parameter (21 per tick)'
  $r = J POST /cheats/dmg/value @{ value = '7' }
  Check (-not $r.active -and (Dmg) -eq 7) 'slider at off_value removes the patch'
  $r = J POST /cheats/dmgsel/step @{ dir = 1 }
  Check ($r.active -and (Dmg) -eq 1) 'select: first option applied by "next" (1 per tick)'
  J POST /cheats/dmgsel/step @{ dir = 1 } | Out-Null
  Check ((Dmg) -eq 100) 'select: next option (100 per tick)'
  J POST /cheats/off | Out-Null
  Check ((Dmg) -eq 7) 'all off restores the original code'
  $s = J GET /state
  $dmgC = $s.cheats | Where-Object id -eq 'dmg'
  Check ($dmgC.value -eq '7' -and $dmgC.kind -eq 'slider' -and $dmgC.warn -eq 'test') 'slider position is remembered and exposed'

  $p = J POST /games/dummy/cheats/god/pin
  $godC = (J GET /state).cheats | Where-Object id -eq 'god'
  Check ($p.pinned -and $godC.pinned) 'pin is saved'
  J PUT /games/dummy/cheats/ammo @{ value = ''; hotkey = 'F8'; hotkey_down = 'CTRL+SHIFT+F8' } | Out-Null
  $ammoC = (J GET /state).cheats | Where-Object id -eq 'ammo'
  Check ($ammoC.hotkey -eq 'F8' -and $ammoC.hotkey_down -eq 'CTRL+SHIFT+F8') 'second hotkey is saved'

  # Restart the game: cheats must resolve from saved chains without calibration
  Stop-Process -Id $g.Id; Start-Sleep 1.5
  $g = Start-Process $dummy -PassThru -WindowStyle Hidden -RedirectStandardOutput $out
  Start-Sleep 4
  $s = J GET /state
  $hpC = $s.cheats | Where-Object id -eq 'hp'
  Check ($s.attached.pid -eq $g.Id) 're-attached after restart'
  Check ($hpC.status -eq 'ready' -and [math]::Abs([int]$hpC.current - (Hp)) -le 7) "HP resolves after restart (cheat sees $($hpC.current), game shows $(Hp))"
  J POST /cheats/hp/toggle | Out-Null
  Start-Sleep 3
  $hp1 = Hp; Start-Sleep 2; $hp2 = Hp
  Check ($hp1 -eq $hp2) "freeze holds HP after restart ($hp1 = $hp2)"
  $r = WaitJob (J POST /calib/hp/bind @{ addr = $hpC.addr; vtype = 'i32' })
  Check ($r.how -eq 'confirmed' -and $r.confirmations -eq 2) "re-calibration confirms chains ($($r.chains) left, confirmations $($r.confirmations))"

  J PUT /games/dummy/cheats/ammo @{ value = '555'; hotkey = 'CTRL+F6' } | Out-Null
  $c = (@(J GET /games | ForEach-Object { $_ }) | Where-Object id -eq 'dummy').cheats | Where-Object id -eq 'ammo'
  Check ($c.value -eq '555' -and $c.hotkey -eq 'CTRL+F6') 'settings override saved'
  $rejected = $false; try { J PUT /games/dummy/cheats/ammo @{ value = 'abc'; hotkey = '' } | Out-Null } catch { $rejected = $true }
  Check $rejected 'invalid value rejected'
} finally {
  Get-Process fahrenhack, dummygame2 -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue | Stop-Process -Force
  Remove-Item Env:FAHRENHACK_DATA -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
}
Check (-not (Get-ChildItem $env:TEMP -Filter 'fahrenhack-*.snap')) 'no snapshot files left in TEMP'
if ($fail) { "$fail check(s) FAILED"; exit 1 } else { 'ALL CHECKS PASSED' }

```

`tools/window-check.ps1`:

```ps1
# Checks the real desktop window (WebView2), not a browser: UI loaded, window buttons, optional real mouse drag.
#   powershell -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File tools\window-check.ps1 [-Exe path] [-Drag]
# Uses a temporary data dir, so the user's %APPDATA%\FahrenHack is untouched.
# -Drag moves the real mouse cursor for about a second (it is put back afterwards).
param([string]$Exe = "$PSScriptRoot\..\target\release\fahrenhack.exe", [switch]$Drag)
$ErrorActionPreference = 'Stop'
. "$PSScriptRoot\cdp.ps1"
if (-not ('FhW32' -as [type])) {
Add-Type @"
using System; using System.Runtime.InteropServices;
public static class FhW32 {
  [StructLayout(LayoutKind.Sequential)] public struct RECT { public int L, T, R, B; }
  [StructLayout(LayoutKind.Sequential)] public struct POINT { public int X, Y; }
  [DllImport("user32.dll")] public static extern bool GetWindowRect(IntPtr h, out RECT r);
  [DllImport("user32.dll")] public static extern bool ClientToScreen(IntPtr h, ref POINT p);
  [DllImport("user32.dll")] public static extern bool IsZoomed(IntPtr h);
  [DllImport("user32.dll")] public static extern bool IsIconic(IntPtr h);
  [DllImport("user32.dll")] public static extern bool ShowWindow(IntPtr h, int cmd);
  [DllImport("user32.dll")] public static extern bool SetWindowPos(IntPtr h, IntPtr after, int x, int y, int cx, int cy, uint flags);
  [DllImport("user32.dll")] public static extern IntPtr WindowFromPoint(POINT p);
  [DllImport("user32.dll")] public static extern IntPtr GetAncestor(IntPtr h, uint flags);
  [DllImport("user32.dll")] public static extern bool SetCursorPos(int x, int y);
  [DllImport("user32.dll")] public static extern bool GetCursorPos(out POINT p);
  [DllImport("user32.dll")] public static extern void mouse_event(uint f, uint dx, uint dy, uint d, UIntPtr e);
  [DllImport("user32.dll")] public static extern uint GetDpiForWindow(IntPtr h);
}
"@
}
function Rect($h) { $r = New-Object FhW32+RECT; [void][FhW32]::GetWindowRect($h, [ref]$r); $r }

$Exe = (Resolve-Path $Exe).Path
Get-Process fahrenhack -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue | Stop-Process -Force
Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 800
$data = Join-Path $env:TEMP 'fahrenhack-window-check'
$env:FAHRENHACK_DATA = $data
$env:WEBVIEW2_ADDITIONAL_BROWSER_ARGUMENTS = '--remote-debugging-port=9223'
$p = Start-Process $Exe -PassThru
try {
  $ws = Get-CdpPage 9223
  Start-Sleep 3
  "UI: " + (Invoke-CdpEval $ws "JSON.stringify({ desktopClass: document.body.classList.contains('desktop'), view: document.body.dataset.view, clock: document.querySelector('#clock').textContent, tiles: document.querySelectorAll('#shelf .tile').length, hints: document.querySelectorAll('#hints .hint').length, winButtons: [...document.querySelectorAll('.winctl button')].filter(b => b.offsetWidth > 0).length })")
  $shot = Join-Path $env:TEMP 'fahrenhack-window.png'
  Save-CdpScreenshot $ws $shot
  "screenshot: $shot"

  $h = [IntPtr]::Zero
  for ($i = 0; $i -lt 20 -and $h -eq [IntPtr]::Zero; $i++) { $p.Refresh(); $h = $p.MainWindowHandle; Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 200 }

  if ($Drag) {
    # Real mouse drag on an empty spot of the header, only if that spot belongs to our window.
    $s = [FhW32]::GetDpiForWindow($h) / 96.0
    $o = New-Object FhW32+POINT; [void][FhW32]::ClientToScreen($h, [ref]$o)
    $cssX = 300; $cssY = 33
    "element at header point: " + (Invoke-CdpEval $ws "(() => { const e = document.elementFromPoint($cssX, $cssY); return e ? e.tagName + (e.id ? '#' + e.id : '') + (e.closest('button') ? ' (inside button!)' : '') : null })()")
    [void][FhW32]::SetWindowPos($h, [IntPtr](-1), 0, 0, 0, 0, 0x0001 -bor 0x0002 -bor 0x0040)
    Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 300
    $pt = New-Object FhW32+POINT; $pt.X = [int]($o.X + $cssX * $s); $pt.Y = [int]($o.Y + $cssY * $s)
    $root = [FhW32]::GetAncestor([FhW32]::WindowFromPoint($pt), 2)
    if ($root -ne $h) { "SKIP drag: point ($($pt.X),$($pt.Y)) is not over the FahrenHack window" }
    else {
      $saved = New-Object FhW32+POINT; [void][FhW32]::GetCursorPos([ref]$saved)
      $r0 = Rect $h
      [void][FhW32]::SetCursorPos($pt.X, $pt.Y); Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 150
      [FhW32]::mouse_event(0x0002, 0, 0, 0, [UIntPtr]::Zero); Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 200
      for ($k = 1; $k -le 12; $k++) { [void][FhW32]::SetCursorPos($pt.X + 12 * $k, $pt.Y + 6 * $k); Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 25 }
      Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 150
      [FhW32]::mouse_event(0x0004, 0, 0, 0, [UIntPtr]::Zero); Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 400
      $r1 = Rect $h
      [void][FhW32]::SetCursorPos($saved.X, $saved.Y)
      "drag: window moved by ($($r1.L - $r0.L), $($r1.T - $r0.T)) px, expected about (144, 72)"
    }
    [void][FhW32]::SetWindowPos($h, [IntPtr](-2), 0, 0, 0, 0, 0x0001 -bor 0x0002)
  }

  # Window buttons use the same IPC path (window.ipc.postMessage) as dragging.
  [void](Invoke-CdpEval $ws "window.ipc.postMessage('max')"); Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 700
  "after 'max': zoomed=$([FhW32]::IsZoomed($h))"
  [void](Invoke-CdpEval $ws "window.ipc.postMessage('max')"); Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 700
  "after 'max' again: zoomed=$([FhW32]::IsZoomed($h))"
  [void](Invoke-CdpEval $ws "window.ipc.postMessage('min')"); Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 700
  "after 'min': minimized=$([FhW32]::IsIconic($h))"
} finally {
  Stop-Process -Id $p.Id -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
  Remove-Item Env:WEBVIEW2_ADDITIONAL_BROWSER_ARGUMENTS -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
  Remove-Item Env:FAHRENHACK_DATA -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
}

```
